# PlexUI for Vue: full reference
> PlexUI for Vue 3: the same components, props, markup and styles as the React library.

Vue 3 components with the same names, props, defaults and markup as the React library. Quick overview: https://plexui.com/llms-vue.txt

Install: npm install @plexui/vue @plexui/styles
Docs: https://plexui.com
Components: 50

## Setup

```css
@layer theme, base, components, utilities;
@import "tailwindcss";
@import "@plexui/styles/css";
@source "../node_modules/@plexui/vue";
```

Wrap your app with <PlexUIProvider :link-component="RouterLink"> from "@plexui/vue/components/PlexUIProvider"

Size scale: 3xs, 2xs, xs, sm, md, lg, xl, 2xl, 3xl
Colors: primary, secondary, danger, success, warning, caution, discovery, info
Variants: solid, soft, outline, ghost

---

## Accordion
Vertically stacked panels that expand to reveal content, with one or several items open at a time and full keyboard navigation.
Import: import { Accordion, AccordionContent, AccordionItem, AccordionTrigger } from "@plexui/vue/components/Accordion"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/accordion
Props:
  - type ("single" | "multiple") — Required. Whether one item or many can be open at a time
  - v-model:value / value (string | string[]) — Controlled open item(s), named like the React prop
  - default-value (string | string[]) — Uncontrolled initial open item(s)
  - collapsible (boolean) default: false — When type="single", allow closing the open item
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable every item
  - dir ("ltr" | "rtl") default: "ltr" — Reading direction
  - orientation ("horizontal" | "vertical") default: "vertical" — Layout axis
  - class (string) — Applied to the root <div>
  - @update:value ((value: string | string[]) => void) — Fires when the open item(s) change; closing the open item of a single accordion reports ""
  AccordionItem:
    - value (string) — Required. Unique identifier used by value / default-value
    - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable this item
    - class (string) — Applied to the item wrapper

### Overview
```vue
<Accordion type="single" collapsible default-value="shipping" style="width: 100%; max-width: 384px">
  <AccordionItem value="shipping">
    <AccordionTrigger>What are your shipping options?</AccordionTrigger>
    <AccordionContent>
      We offer standard (5–7 business days), express (2–3 days), and
      overnight shipping. Free standard shipping on orders over $50.
    </AccordionContent>
  </AccordionItem>
  <AccordionItem value="returns">
    <AccordionTrigger>What is your return policy?</AccordionTrigger>
    <AccordionContent>
      Returns are accepted within 30 days of delivery. Items must be unused
      and in original packaging. Refunds are processed within 5–7 business
      days of receipt.
    </AccordionContent>
  </AccordionItem>
  <AccordionItem value="support">
    <AccordionTrigger>How can I contact customer support?</AccordionTrigger>
    <AccordionContent>
      Reach us by email, live chat, or phone Monday through Friday, 9am–6pm
      ET. Weekend tickets are handled within 24 hours.
    </AccordionContent>
  </AccordionItem>
</Accordion>
```

### Single
Default shape. type="single" + collapsible lets users close the currently open item; without collapsible, one item is always open.
```vue
<Accordion type="single" collapsible default-value="trial" style="width: 100%; max-width: 384px">
  <AccordionItem
    v-for="item in [
      {
        value: 'trial',
        trigger: 'How long is the free trial?',
        content: '14 days, no credit card required. At the end of the trial you can keep the free tier or upgrade to Pro; your projects stay in place either way.',
      },
      {
        value: 'plan',
        trigger: 'Can I change plans mid-cycle?',
        content: 'Yes. Upgrades are prorated immediately; downgrades take effect at the next billing date. Invoices always reflect the exact span each plan was active.',
      },
      {
        value: 'invoices',
        trigger: 'Where can I find past invoices?',
        content: 'Billing → Invoices in your workspace settings. Every PDF is archived for seven years and can be pulled via the Billing API.',
      },
    ]"
    :key="item.value"
    :value="item.value"
  >
    <AccordionTrigger>{{ item.trigger }}</AccordionTrigger>
    <AccordionContent>{{ item.content }}</AccordionContent>
  </AccordionItem>
</Accordion>
```

### Multiple
Use type="multiple" when readers can keep several sections open at once: settings pages, long FAQs, step-by-step references.
```vue
<Accordion type="multiple" :default-value="['notifications']" style="width: 100%; max-width: 384px">
  <AccordionItem
    v-for="item in [
      {
        value: 'notifications',
        trigger: 'Notification settings',
        content: 'Pick which events trigger email, push, or in-app alerts. Release-blocker incidents always page the on-call regardless of quiet hours.',
      },
      {
        value: 'privacy',
        trigger: 'Privacy & security',
        content: 'Turn on two-factor, review active sessions, and manage connected apps. Workspace admins can also enforce SSO and hardware-key policies org-wide.',
      },
      {
        value: 'billing',
        trigger: 'Billing & subscription',
        content: 'View your current plan, payment method, and upcoming renewal. Seat changes apply immediately; the next invoice will include a prorated adjustment.',
      },
    ]"
    :key="item.value"
    :value="item.value"
  >
    <AccordionTrigger>{{ item.trigger }}</AccordionTrigger>
    <AccordionContent>{{ item.content }}</AccordionContent>
  </AccordionItem>
</Accordion>
```

### Disabled
Pass disabled to an AccordionItem to make it read-only. The row stays visible so the reader understands the section exists; keyboard focus skips it.
```vue
<Accordion type="single" collapsible default-value="history" style="width: 100%; max-width: 384px">
  <AccordionItem value="history">
    <AccordionTrigger>Can I see my usage history?</AccordionTrigger>
    <AccordionContent>
      Yes, the Activity tab logs every API call, deploy, and admin action
      for the last 90 days. Longer retention requires the Enterprise plan.
    </AccordionContent>
  </AccordionItem>
  <AccordionItem value="audit" disabled>
    <AccordionTrigger>Audit log export (Enterprise only)</AccordionTrigger>
    <AccordionContent>
      Upgrade to Enterprise to stream audit events to S3, Splunk, or your
      SIEM of choice.
    </AccordionContent>
  </AccordionItem>
  <AccordionItem value="two-factor">
    <AccordionTrigger>How do I enable two-factor auth?</AccordionTrigger>
    <AccordionContent>
      Settings → Security → Two-factor. You can use an authenticator app or
      a FIDO2 hardware key. Recovery codes are issued once at enrollment.
    </AccordionContent>
  </AccordionItem>
</Accordion>
```

### Card
Composed inside Card, the most common landing-page FAQ pattern.
```vue
<Card style="width: 100%; max-width: 384px">
  <CardHeader>
    <CardTitle>Subscription & billing</CardTitle>
    <CardDescription>Common questions about plans, invoices, and cancellations.</CardDescription>
  </CardHeader>
  <CardContent>
    <Accordion type="single" collapsible default-value="plans">
      <AccordionItem
        v-for="item in [
          {
            value: 'plans',
            trigger: 'What subscription plans do you offer?',
            content: 'Starter ($9/mo), Pro ($29/mo), and Enterprise (from $99/mo). Each tier raises seat, project, and API call limits; Enterprise adds SSO, audit log export, and a dedicated success manager.',
          },
          {
            value: 'billing',
            trigger: 'How does billing work?',
            content: 'Billed at the start of each cycle. We accept all major cards, and ACH or invoice for annual Enterprise contracts. Every invoice is emailed and archived in Billing → Invoices.',
          },
          {
            value: 'cancel',
            trigger: 'How do I cancel my subscription?',
            content: 'Cancel anytime from Billing → Plan. No cancellation fee. Access continues through the end of the current billing period, and projects stay read-only afterwards. Nothing is deleted.',
          },
        ]"
        :key="item.value"
        :value="item.value"
      >
        <AccordionTrigger>{{ item.trigger }}</AccordionTrigger>
        <AccordionContent>{{ item.content }}</AccordionContent>
      </AccordionItem>
    </Accordion>
  </CardContent>
</Card>
```

## Alert
Inline message for notices and warnings with semantic colors, variants, a title, a description, action slots, and a dismiss control.
Import: import { Alert } from "@plexui/vue/components/Alert"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/alert
Props:
  - title (string | slot) — Title displayed in the alert
  - description (string | slot) — Description text
  - actions (slot) — Actions (for example buttons)
  - actions-placement ("end" | "bottom") — Placement of actions; automatic from sizing when unset
  - actions-class-name (string) — Class applied to the actions container
  - color (SemanticColors) default: "primary" — Color scheme
  - variant ("solid" | "soft" | "outline") default: "outline" — Style variant
  - indicator (slot | false) — Custom indicator (slot), or :indicator="false" to hide it
  - @dismiss (() => void) — Shows the dismiss button when set
  - banner (boolean) default: false — Full-width strip: square corners, lines above and below only

### Overview
```vue
<Alert
  title="Scheduled maintenance"
  description="We'll be offline 2 - 4 AM UTC on July 14 while we upgrade our database."
>
  <template #actions>
    <Button color="primary" variant="soft" pill>Dismiss</Button>
  </template>
</Alert>
```

### Colors & variants
You can stylize the display of the alert using the color and variant props.
```vue
<div class="w-fit min-w-full max-w-[500px]">
  <div class="flex flex-col gap-6">
    <div
      v-for="color in ['primary', 'success', 'danger', 'warning', 'caution', 'info', 'discovery']"
      :key="color"
      class="flex items-center"
    >
      <div class="text-right text-sm mr-8 min-w-[4rem]" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary)">{{ color }}</div>
      <div class="flex-1">
        <Alert :color="color" :variant="variant" title="Scheduled maintenance on Saturday, 2-4 AM PT">
          <template #description>
            Track status updates on <TextLink color="currentcolor" href="#">status page</TextLink>
          </template>
        </Alert>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
```

### Title
You can optionally include a title for your alert with the title prop (or the title slot).
```vue
<Alert title="Your current access level limits what you can view or modify" />
```

### Description
You can optionally include description text for your alert with the description prop, or the description slot for rich content.
```vue
<Alert>
  <template #description>
    We're working on centralizing SCIM and invite settings. For now, setup is handled within individual product settings. <TextLink href="#">Learn more</TextLink>
  </template>
</Alert>
```

### Actions
You can add actions to the alert with the actions slot. Combine with @dismiss to add a dismiss button.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-6 w-full">
  <Alert
    variant="soft"
    color="warning"
    title="Password expires in 3 days"
    description="Update it now to avoid losing access to your account."
  >
    <template #actions>
      <Button color="primary" pill>Update password</Button>
    </template>
  </Alert>
  <Alert
    variant="soft"
    color="warning"
    title="Your API key will be rotated soon"
    description="Automatic key rotation is scheduled for March 10. Download the new key afterward to keep your integrations running."
    @dismiss="() => {}"
  >
    <template #actions>
      <Button color="warning" variant="soft" pill>Remind me later</Button>
    </template>
  </Alert>
</div>
```

### Action placement
Actions are automatically positioned in the alert by default. You can force a specific position with the actions-placement prop by passing either 'end' or 'bottom'.
```vue
<Alert
  title="Our terms of service has been updated"
  description="We've updated our terms to clarify how we handle data, billing, and user permissions. Please review and accept the latest terms to avoid impacting your service."
  actions-placement="bottom"
>
  <template #actions>
    <Button color="primary" variant="soft" pill>Set reminder</Button>
    <Button color="primary" variant="solid" pill>Review terms</Button>
  </template>
</Alert>
```

### Indicator
Alert provides an indicator icon by default. You can override it with the indicator slot, or pass :indicator="false" to hide it entirely.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-6 w-full">
  <Alert>
    <template #indicator>
      <Lightbulb />
    </template>
    <template #description>
      We're working on centralizing SCIM and invite settings. For now, setup is handled within individual product settings. <TextLink href="#">Learn more</TextLink>
    </template>
  </Alert>
  <Alert
    :indicator="false"
    description="Version 2.18.5 rolls out behind-the-scenes tweaks to caching and background sync. You don't need to do anything."
  />
</div>
```

### Dismissible
The suggested approach for dismissible notices is to use labeled buttons in the actions slot. You can also listen to @dismiss to render a dismiss button in the alert chrome.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-6 w-full">
  <Alert
    class="items-start"
    title="Try our new dashboard layout"
    description="We've introduced a streamlined layout that makes using the dashboard even easier. You can switch back any time."
  >
    <template #actions>
      <Button color="primary" variant="soft" pill>Dismiss</Button>
      <Button color="primary" variant="solid" pill>Try it</Button>
    </template>
  </Alert>
  <Alert
    title="Thank you!"
    description="Your application has been received. We will review your application and respond within the next 48 hours."
    @dismiss="() => {}"
  />
</div>
```

### Banner
Use banner for a full-width strip across the top or bottom of a page or panel: offline, read-only, a new version. It drops the rounded corners and keeps only the lines above and below. Pass :indicator="false" for a plain strip.
```vue
<div class="flex-1 w-full py-12 flex flex-col items-stretch justify-center gap-6">
  <Alert
    banner
    color="caution"
    variant="soft"
    :indicator="false"
    description="You are offline. Changes will sync when the connection is back."
  />
  <Alert banner color="info" variant="outline" description="A new version is available.">
    <template #actions>
      <Button color="primary" variant="soft" size="sm">Reload</Button>
    </template>
  </Alert>
</div>
```

## Avatar
An avatar for user identities that shows an initial, a photo or an icon, with overflow counts, semantic colors and roundness options.
Import: import { Avatar } from "@plexui/vue/components/Avatar"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/avatar
Props:
  - name (string) — Display name; used for text initial when no image/icon
  - image-url (string) — Absolute URL for image avatar
  - Icon (Component) — Icon component for icon avatar, bound as :Icon
  - size (number) — Width and height in pixels
  - color ("primary" | "secondary" | "success" | "info" | "discovery" | "danger") default: "secondary" — Semantic color for text/icon avatar
  - variant ("soft" | "solid") default: "soft" — Style variant
  - overflow-count (number) — Show overflow count (e.g. +9)
  - class (string) — Applied to the avatar root
  - @click ((event: MouseEvent) => void) — Makes avatar interactive (renders a button)
  - @pointerdown ((event: PointerEvent) => void) — Makes avatar interactive (renders a button)

### Overview
A variety of avatar types: text, image, icon, and overflow.
```vue
<div class="flex gap-4">
  <Avatar name="Tyler" image-url="https://gravatar.com/avatar/9531b260b9693f3394bea8646c6ea141ce58fe5a138b7db7729d60a4c5dde552" :size="48" />
  <Avatar name="Will" :size="48" />
  <Avatar name="Tech support" :Icon="Robot" :size="48" color="primary" variant="solid" />
</div>
```

### Text
For text-based avatars, a single initial is displayed based on the name prop passed to the component.
```vue
<Avatar name="David" :size="48" />
```

### Image
To display an image avatar, provide an absolute URL to the image with image-url. Images will have a soft fade entrance once loaded, or fall back to a text display if the image fails to load.
```vue
<Avatar
  name="Tyler"
  image-url="https://gravatar.com/avatar/9531b260b9693f3394bea8646c6ea141ce58fe5a138b7db7729d60a4c5dde552"
  :size="48"
/>
```

### Icon
To display an icon avatar, pass an icon component to the Icon prop, for example Robot imported from @plexui/vue/components/Icon. The name prop is not used in this case, and can be omitted.
```vue
<Avatar
  :Icon="Robot"
  :size="48"
/>
```

### Overflow count
To display an overflow avatar, provide a number to the overflow-count prop. The number will be automatically formatted and scaled to fit.
```vue
<Avatar :overflow-count="overflowCount" :size="size" />
```

### Sizing
To size the avatar, pass a number to the size prop, which will set the width and height in pixels. Text and icon avatars will scale their sizes dynamically based on this value.
```vue
<Avatar name="David" :size="size" />
```

### Colors & variants
You can stylize the display of the avatar using the color and variant props.
```vue
<div
  class="grid items-center gap-x-8 gap-y-5"
  style="grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, auto)) auto; justify-items: center"
>
  <div
    v-for="variant in ['soft', 'solid']"
    :key="variant"
    class="text-sm font-medium"
    style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary)"
  >{{ variant }}</div>
  <div />
  <template v-for="(color, idx) in ['primary', 'secondary', 'success', 'danger', 'info', 'discovery']" :key="color">
    <Avatar
      v-for="variant in ['soft', 'solid']"
      :key="variant"
      :name="String.fromCharCode(65 + idx)"
      :color="color"
      :variant="variant"
      :size="size"
    />
    <div class="text-sm font-medium justify-self-start" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary)">{{ color }}</div>
  </template>
</div>
```

### Roundness
By default, avatars are fully rounded. You can override this by passing a Tailwind rounding class to class.
```vue
<Avatar
  name="Acme, co."
  color="primary"
  variant="solid"
  :size="size"
  :class="'rounded-' + roundness"
/>
```

### Interactive
Avatars can be used as interactive elements by listening to @click (or @pointerdown). The avatar then renders a button and applies semantic and visual interaction styles.
```vue
<div class="flex items-center gap-4">
  <Avatar name="Will" :size="48" @click="saySup" />
  <Avatar
    image-url="https://gravatar.com/avatar/9531b260b9693f3394bea8646c6ea141ce58fe5a138b7db7729d60a4c5dde552"
    name="Tyler"
    :size="48"
    @click="saySup"
  />
  <Avatar name="Tech support" :Icon="Robot" :size="48" color="info" @click="saySup" />
</div>
```

## Avatar Group
Stacks user avatars into a single overlapping cluster, with a configurable stacking direction and one size for the whole group.
Import: import { Avatar, AvatarGroup } from "@plexui/vue/components/Avatar"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/avatar-group
Props:
  - default slot (VNode[]) — The avatars of the group
  - size (number) — Size of all avatars in the group
  - stack ("start" | "end") default: "start" — Stacking direction
  - class (string) — Applied to the group container; other attributes are not forwarded

### Overview
```vue
<AvatarGroup :size="42">
  <Avatar name="Tyler" image-url="https://gravatar.com/avatar/9531b260b9693f3394bea8646c6ea141ce58fe5a138b7db7729d60a4c5dde552" />
  <Avatar name="Jane" color="primary" variant="solid" />
  <Avatar name="Tech support" :Icon="Robot" variant="solid" />
  <Avatar :overflow-count="5" />
</AvatarGroup>
```

### Stacking direction
You can control the stacking direction using the stack prop, as either "start" or "end".
```vue
<AvatarGroup :stack="stack" :size="42">
  <Avatar name="Tyler" image-url="https://gravatar.com/avatar/9531b260b9693f3394bea8646c6ea141ce58fe5a138b7db7729d60a4c5dde552" />
  <Avatar name="Will" />
  <Avatar name="Tech support" :Icon="Robot" variant="solid" />
  <Avatar :overflow-count="5" variant="soft" />
</AvatarGroup>
```

### Group sizing
You can control the size of all avatars in the group by passing size directly to the &lt;AvatarGroup&gt; component.
```vue
<AvatarGroup :size="size">
  <Avatar name="Tyler" color="info" />
  <Avatar name="Jane" color="discovery" />
  <Avatar name="Will" color="danger" />
  <Avatar :overflow-count="5" />
</AvatarGroup>
```

## Badge
Compact label for statuses and inline metadata, with semantic colors, three variants and sizes, icon support, and a loading state.
Import: import { Badge } from "@plexui/vue/components/Badge"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/badge
Props:
  - default slot (VNode[]) — Content inside the badge
  - color ("secondary" | "success" | "danger" | "warning" | "info" | "discovery" | "caution") default: "secondary" — Semantic color
  - variant ("solid" | "soft" | "outline") default: "soft" — Style variant
  - size ("sm" | "md" | "lg") default: "sm" — Badge size
  - pill (boolean) default: false — Fully rounded

### Overview
Status badges in different colors and compositions.
```vue
<div class="flex gap-4 flex-wrap">
  <Badge color="success" size="lg">Successful</Badge>
  <Badge size="lg" class="gap-1.5"><LoadingIndicator /> In progress</Badge>
  <Badge color="danger" size="lg">Failed</Badge>
  <Badge color="warning" size="lg" pill><Flask /> Beta</Badge>
  <Badge color="caution" size="lg">Caution</Badge>
</div>
```

### Colors & variants
You can stylize the display of the badge using the color and variant props.
```vue
<div class="w-full overflow-x-auto">
  <div class="pt-1 pb-6 px-8 w-fit min-w-full">
    <div class="grid gap-6 items-center justify-center" style="grid-template-columns: auto repeat(7, min-content)">
      <div />
      <div
        v-for="color in ['secondary', 'success', 'warning', 'danger', 'info', 'discovery', 'caution']"
        :key="color"
        class="text-center mb-1"
        style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); font-size: 0.875rem"
      >{{ color }}</div>
      <template v-for="variant in ['soft', 'solid', 'outline']" :key="variant">
        <div class="text-right mr-3" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); font-size: 0.875rem">{{ variant }}</div>
        <div
          v-for="color in ['secondary', 'success', 'warning', 'danger', 'info', 'discovery', 'caution']"
          :key="color"
          class="text-center"
        >
          <Badge :color="color" :variant="variant">Sample</Badge>
        </div>
      </template>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
```

### Size & roundness
You can control the size and roundness of the badge with size and pill props. When pill is set, extra horizontal gutter is dynamically applied to the badge.
```vue
<Badge color="info" :size="size" :pill="pill">New</Badge>
```

### Icon
Content within the badge can be composed, like showing icons. Color, sizing, and spacing adapt by default.
```vue
<Badge color="warning" size="lg"><Flask /> Beta</Badge>
```

### Loading
In this example, additional gap is added with a Tailwind gap-1.5 class to provide the appropriate visual spacing.
```vue
<Badge size="lg" color="secondary" class="gap-1.5">
  <LoadingIndicator /> In progress
</Badge>
```

## Breadcrumb
The trail of pages that leads to the current one, above a page title, with links, plain steps and the current page.
Import: import { Breadcrumb, BreadcrumbItem } from "@plexui/vue/components/Breadcrumb"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/breadcrumb
Props:
  - size ("sm" | "md") default: "md" — Text size: sm above a compact header, md above a page title
  - aria-label (string) default: "Breadcrumb" — Name of the navigation landmark
  - separator slot (VNode[]) default: a small chevron — What stands between two items
  - default slot (VNode[]) — The BreadcrumbItem elements
  BreadcrumbItem:
    - href (string) — Link destination. Internal paths use the link component from PlexUIProvider
    - to (string | RouteLocation) — Router destination, for link components that take to
    - current (boolean) default: false — The current page: plain text marked aria-current="page"
    - @click ((event: MouseEvent) => void) — Makes an item without a destination act as a button
    - class (string) — Applied to the li element

### Overview
Breadcrumb renders a nav landmark named "Breadcrumb" around an ordered list. An item with href or to is a link (internal paths use the link component from PlexUIProvider, such as vue-router's RouterLink); the current item is plain text marked aria-current="page".
```vue
<Breadcrumb>
  <BreadcrumbItem href="#">Home</BreadcrumbItem>
  <BreadcrumbItem href="#">Settings</BreadcrumbItem>
  <BreadcrumbItem current>Members</BreadcrumbItem>
</Breadcrumb>
```

### Sizes
Use size="sm" for a trail above a compact header; the default md sits above a page title.
```vue
<Breadcrumb size="sm">
  <BreadcrumbItem href="#">Projects</BreadcrumbItem>
  <BreadcrumbItem href="#">Website</BreadcrumbItem>
  <BreadcrumbItem current>Deployments</BreadcrumbItem>
</Breadcrumb>
<Breadcrumb>
  <BreadcrumbItem href="#">Projects</BreadcrumbItem>
  <BreadcrumbItem href="#">Website</BreadcrumbItem>
  <BreadcrumbItem current>Deployments</BreadcrumbItem>
</Breadcrumb>
```

### Separator
A small chevron stands between items by default. The separator slot replaces it, such as with a slash for file paths. Separators are hidden from assistive technology.
```vue
<Breadcrumb>
  <template #separator>/</template>
  <BreadcrumbItem href="#">acme</BreadcrumbItem>
  <BreadcrumbItem href="#">design-system</BreadcrumbItem>
  <BreadcrumbItem current>README.md</BreadcrumbItem>
</Breadcrumb>
```

### Long trails
A trail that does not fit wraps onto the next line instead of leaving the screen. An item without href is a plain step; listen to @click to make it act as a button.
```vue
<div class="w-[260px]">
  <Breadcrumb>
    <BreadcrumbItem href="#">Workspace</BreadcrumbItem>
    <BreadcrumbItem href="#">Customer accounts</BreadcrumbItem>
    <BreadcrumbItem href="#">Northwind Traders</BreadcrumbItem>
    <BreadcrumbItem>Archived</BreadcrumbItem>
    <BreadcrumbItem current>Invoice 2024-0193</BreadcrumbItem>
  </Breadcrumb>
</div>
```

## Button
Button with four variants (solid, soft, outline, ghost), eight semantic colors, icon slots, loading, and block layout.
Import: import { Button } from "@plexui/vue/components/Button"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/button
Props:
  - color ("primary" | "secondary" | "danger" | "success" | "info" | "discovery" | "caution" | "warning") default: "primary" — Visual color scheme
  - variant ("solid" | "soft" | "outline" | "ghost") default: "solid" — Button style variant
  - size ("3xs" | "2xs" | "xs" | "sm" | "md" | "lg" | "xl" | "2xl" | "3xl") default: "md" — Control height and default gutter/icon size
  - pill (boolean) default: false — Fully rounded corners
  - block (boolean) default: false — Full width
  - uniform (boolean) default: false — Square icon button (width equals height)
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Visually and accessibly disabled; click is not emitted
  - disabled-tone ("relaxed") — Default cursor instead of not-allowed while disabled
  - loading (boolean) default: false — Shows loading indicator
  - inert (boolean) default: loading — Accessibly disabled without visual change
  - selected (boolean) default: false — Selected state styling
  - optically-align ("start" | "end") — Offset gutter for optical alignment
  - gutter-size ("3xs" | "2xs" | "xs" | "sm" | "md" | "lg" | "xl") — Horizontal padding size
  - icon-size ("sm" | "md" | "lg" | "xl" | "2xl") — Size of icons in the slot
  - type ("button" | "submit" | "reset") default: "button" — Native button type
  - @click ((event: MouseEvent) => void) — Emitted on click, unless disabled

### Overview
A showcase of Button's key styles: solid, soft, outline, ghost, and icon-only.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
  <Button color="primary">Submit</Button>
  <Button variant="soft"><CompareArrows /> Compare</Button>
  <Button color="danger" gutter-size="sm" :pill="false"><Delete /></Button>
  <Button variant="outline">Next <ArrowRight /></Button>
  <Button variant="ghost" color="secondary" uniform><Clear /></Button>
</div>
```

### Colors & variants
You can stylize the display of the button using the color and variant props.
```vue
<div class="w-[calc(100%+48px)] -mx-6 overflow-x-auto [scrollbar-width:none] [-ms-overflow-style:none] [&::-webkit-scrollbar]:hidden">
  <div class="pt-1 pb-6 px-8 w-max mx-auto">
    <div class="grid gap-6 items-center justify-center" style="grid-template-columns: auto repeat(8, min-content)">
      <div />
      <div
        v-for="color in ['primary', 'secondary', 'danger', 'info', 'discovery', 'success', 'caution', 'warning']"
        :key="color"
        class="text-center text-sm mb-1"
        style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary)"
      >{{ color }}</div>
      <template v-for="variant in ['soft', 'solid', 'outline', 'ghost']" :key="variant">
        <div class="text-right text-sm mr-3" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary)">{{ variant }}</div>
        <div
          v-for="color in ['primary', 'secondary', 'danger', 'info', 'discovery', 'success', 'caution', 'warning']"
          :key="color"
          class="text-center"
        >
          <Button :color="color" :variant="variant" size="md">Submit</Button>
        </div>
      </template>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
```

### Sizing & roundness
Control height with size and horizontal padding with gutter-size. Use pill for fully rounded corners. Each size provides default values for gutter-size and icon-size, as well as font-size. Each of these values can be overridden independently.
```vue
<Button :color="color" :variant="variant" :size="size" :pill="pill"><Mail /> Button <ArrowRight /></Button>
<Button :color="color" :variant="variant" :size="size" :pill="pill">Button <ArrowRight /></Button>
<Button :color="color" :variant="variant" :size="size" :pill="pill"><Mail /> Button</Button>
<Button :color="color" :variant="variant" :size="size" :pill="pill">Button</Button>
```

### Icons
Add icons to the default slot. Sizing is automatic via the icon-size prop. For square icon buttons use uniform, which ignores gutter-size. Combine it with pill to make circular icons.
```vue
<Button
  :color="color"
  :size="size"
  :gutter-size="gutterSize === 'auto' ? undefined : gutterSize"
  :icon-size="iconSize === 'auto' ? undefined : iconSize"
  :uniform="uniform"
  :variant="variant"
>
  <PlusLg />
</Button>
```

### Block
Create full-width buttons with the block prop.
```vue
<div class="w-[290px] mx-auto text-center p-2 border border-dashed border-alpha/20 rounded-md">
  <Button color="primary" size="lg" :block="block">Continue</Button>
</div>
```

### Disabled
Use disabled to both visually and accessibly disable a button. Click events are not emitted.
```vue
<Button color="primary" :disabled="disabled">Submit</Button>
```

### Inert
Use inert to accessibly disable a button without any visual changes.
```vue
<Button color="primary" :inert="inert">Submit</Button>
```

### Loading
Use loading to display a loading indicator in the button, fading out the current content. Interactions are prevented during this state, because inert defaults to loading. Override it with inert, or use disabled to add visual disablement.
```vue
<Button loading size="xl" pill><ArrowUp /> Click to load</Button>
```

### Selected
Use selected to show that a button is in an open or selected state. The exact visual behavior differs across each variant. This behavior is automatically configured with components like Menu that apply [aria-expanded="true"] or [data-state="open"] attributes.
```vue
<Popover>
  <PopoverTrigger>
    <Button color="primary" :selected="selected" :variant="variant">Click to open</Button>
  </PopoverTrigger>
  <PopoverContent min-width="auto" class="p-4 text-sm">
    Button should look selected
  </PopoverContent>
</Popover>
```

### Optical alignment
Use optically-align with either "start" or "end" to offset the gutter of the button.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-3">
  <div class="border border-dashed border-alpha/20 rounded-md py-4 px-6">
    <div class="mb-2 text-secondary text-sm">Default gutters</div>
    <Button variant="ghost">Ghost button</Button>
  </div>
  <div class="border border-dashed border-alpha/20 rounded-md py-4 px-6">
    <div class="mb-2 text-secondary text-sm">optically-align="start"</div>
    <Button variant="ghost" optically-align="start">Ghost button</Button>
  </div>
</div>
```

## Button Group
Join buttons into a single visual unit for toolbars, steppers, split buttons and search affordances.
Import: import { ButtonGroup, ButtonGroupSeparator, ButtonGroupText } from "@plexui/vue/components/ButtonGroup"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/button-group
Props:
  - orientation ("horizontal" | "vertical") default: "horizontal" — Direction of the joined items
  - class (string) — Applied to the root <div>
  ButtonGroupText:
    - as-child (boolean) default: false — Render the slotted child (e.g. a <label> tied to an input) instead of a <div>
    - class (string) — Applied to the wrapper

### Overview
```vue
<ButtonGroup>
  <ButtonGroup>
    <Button variant="outline" color="secondary" :size="size" uniform aria-label="Go back">
      <ArrowLeftSm />
    </Button>
  </ButtonGroup>
  <ButtonGroup>
    <Button variant="outline" color="secondary" :size="size">Pin</Button>
    <Button variant="outline" color="secondary" :size="size">Tag</Button>
  </ButtonGroup>
  <ButtonGroup>
    <Button variant="outline" color="secondary" :size="size">Bookmark</Button>
    <Button variant="outline" color="secondary" :size="size" uniform aria-label="More options">
      <DotsHorizontal />
    </Button>
  </ButtonGroup>
</ButtonGroup>
```

### Orientation
Pass orientation="vertical" to stack the buttons, useful for + / − steppers, zoom controls, or any vertical toolbar.
```vue
<ButtonGroup orientation="vertical" aria-label="Zoom controls">
  <Button variant="outline" color="secondary" :size="size" uniform aria-label="Zoom in">
    <PlusSm />
  </Button>
  <Button variant="outline" color="secondary" :size="size" uniform aria-label="Zoom out">
    <Minus />
  </Button>
</ButtonGroup>
```

### Size
Set size on every Button inside the group so corner radii match. Mix icon-only (uniform) with text buttons; each child keeps its own padding.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col items-start gap-6">
  <ButtonGroup>
    <Button variant="outline" color="secondary" size="sm">Small</Button>
    <Button variant="outline" color="secondary" size="sm">Button</Button>
    <Button variant="outline" color="secondary" size="sm">Group</Button>
    <Button variant="outline" color="secondary" size="sm" uniform aria-label="Add"><PlusSm /></Button>
  </ButtonGroup>
  <ButtonGroup>
    <Button variant="outline" color="secondary">Default</Button>
    <Button variant="outline" color="secondary">Button</Button>
    <Button variant="outline" color="secondary">Group</Button>
    <Button variant="outline" color="secondary" uniform aria-label="Add"><PlusSm /></Button>
  </ButtonGroup>
  <ButtonGroup>
    <Button variant="outline" color="secondary" size="lg">Large</Button>
    <Button variant="outline" color="secondary" size="lg">Button</Button>
    <Button variant="outline" color="secondary" size="lg">Group</Button>
    <Button variant="outline" color="secondary" size="lg" uniform aria-label="Add"><PlusSm /></Button>
  </ButtonGroup>
</div>
```

### Nested
Place ButtonGroup inside ButtonGroup to create visually separated sub-groups. The outer group automatically adds a small gap between children; pagination with arrow navigation is the canonical pattern.
```vue
<ButtonGroup aria-label="Pager">
  <ButtonGroup>
    <Button v-for="page in 5" :key="page" variant="outline" color="secondary" :size="size">{{ page }}</Button>
  </ButtonGroup>
  <ButtonGroup>
    <Button variant="outline" color="secondary" :size="size" uniform aria-label="Previous page">
      <ArrowLeftSm />
    </Button>
    <Button variant="outline" color="secondary" :size="size" uniform aria-label="Next page">
      <ArrowRightSm />
    </Button>
  </ButtonGroup>
</ButtonGroup>
```

### Separator
Drop a ButtonGroupSeparator between items to add a visible divider, commonly used for Undo / Redo pairs or any "two related actions" toolbar.
```vue
<ButtonGroup>
  <Button variant="soft" color="secondary" :size="size"><Undo /> Undo</Button>
  <ButtonGroupSeparator />
  <Button variant="soft" color="secondary" :size="size">Redo <Redo /></Button>
</ButtonGroup>
```

### Split
The classic split button: an action joined to a secondary affordance via ButtonGroupSeparator. Useful for Save / Save-As, Filter / Add-filter, Delete / Undo.
```vue
<ButtonGroup>
  <Button variant="soft" color="secondary" :size="size"><Filter /> Filter</Button>
  <ButtonGroupSeparator />
  <Button variant="soft" color="secondary" :size="size" uniform aria-label="Add filter">
    <Plus />
  </Button>
</ButtonGroup>
```

### Input
Join an Input to a trailing Button for inline search, token entry, or URL shorteners. The input flexes to fill the row; the button hugs its content.
```vue
<ButtonGroup>
  <Input placeholder="Search docs..." :size="size" />
  <Button variant="outline" color="secondary" :size="size" aria-label="Search">
    <SearchSm />
  </Button>
</ButtonGroup>
```

### Input group
Richer composition for message composers: wrap each slot (leading action, flexible Input) in its own ButtonGroup so the outer group inserts a gap between them. The voice toggle rides inside the input via the endAdornment slot. Use InputAdornmentButton: it sizes off the input's own size token so the button stays concentric with the pill at every size.
```vue
<ButtonGroup style="width: 100%; max-width: 280px">
  <ButtonGroup>
    <Button variant="outline" color="secondary" pill :size="size" uniform aria-label="Add attachment">
      <Plus />
    </Button>
  </ButtonGroup>
  <ButtonGroup>
    <Input
      pill
      :size="size"
      :placeholder="voiceEnabled ? 'Recording your idea…' : 'Describe a component…'"
      :disabled="voiceEnabled"
    >
      <template #endAdornment>
        <InputAdornmentButton
          :color="voiceEnabled ? 'caution' : undefined"
          aria-label="Toggle voice mode"
          :aria-pressed="voiceEnabled"
          @click="voiceEnabled = !voiceEnabled"
        >
          <Mic />
        </InputAdornmentButton>
      </template>
    </Input>
  </ButtonGroup>
</ButtonGroup>
```

### Dropdown menu
Compose a primary action with a Menu trigger for follow-up options. Common for Export / Format, Save / Save-As, Share / ….
```vue
<ButtonGroup>
  <Button variant="outline" color="secondary" :pill="false" :size="size">Export</Button>
  <Menu>
    <MenuTrigger>
      <Button variant="outline" color="secondary" :pill="false" :size="size" uniform aria-label="More export options">
        <ChevronDownMd />
      </Button>
    </MenuTrigger>
    <MenuContent align="end" :min-width="200">
      <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><FileImage /> PNG image</MenuItem>
      <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><FileCode /> SVG vector</MenuItem>
      <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><FileDocument /> PDF document</MenuItem>
      <MenuSeparator />
      <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><Copy /> Copy as code</MenuItem>
      <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><Link /> Copy share link</MenuItem>
    </MenuContent>
  </Menu>
</ButtonGroup>
```

### Select
Mix a Select and an Input inside one ButtonGroup for unit pickers, currency entry, or any "qualifier + value" pattern. Wrap each slot in a nested ButtonGroup to get a gap between the compound field and the send affordance.
```vue
<ButtonGroup>
  <ButtonGroup>
    <Select
      :options="unitOptions"
      v-model:value="unit"
      variant="outline"
      :size="size"
      :block="false"
    />
    <Input placeholder="2.50" :size="size" inputmode="decimal" />
  </ButtonGroup>
  <ButtonGroup>
    <Button variant="outline" color="secondary" :size="size" uniform aria-label="Log weight">
      <ArrowRight />
    </Button>
  </ButtonGroup>
</ButtonGroup>
```

### Popover
Same split-button shape, but the trailing trigger opens a Popover instead of a menu, useful for richer panels (prompt composers, notification preferences, link previews).
```vue
<ButtonGroup>
  <Button variant="outline" color="secondary" :pill="false" :size="size"><Sparkles /> Generate</Button>
  <Popover>
    <PopoverTrigger>
      <Button variant="outline" color="secondary" :pill="false" :size="size" uniform aria-label="More generate options">
        <ChevronDownMd />
      </Button>
    </PopoverTrigger>
    <PopoverContent :min-width="320" side="bottom" align="end" class="p-4">
      <Card variant="ghost" size="sm">
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Generate a component</CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>Describe what to build in plain English.</CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <Textarea placeholder="A login form with email and password…" :rows="3" />
        </CardContent>
        <CardFooter>
          <CardDescription>Plex will draft the code and preview it live.</CardDescription>
        </CardFooter>
      </Card>
    </PopoverContent>
  </Popover>
</ButtonGroup>
```

## Button Link
A semantic anchor with full button styling, for internal and external links with icons and block layout.
Import: import { ButtonLink } from "@plexui/vue/components/Button"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/button-link
Props:
  - default slot (VNode[]) — Content inside the link button
  - color ("primary" | "secondary" | "danger" | "success" | "info" | "discovery" | "caution" | "warning") default: "primary" — Visual color
  - variant ("solid" | "soft" | "outline" | "ghost") default: "solid" — Style variant
  - size ("3xs" | "2xs" | "xs" | "sm" | "md" | "lg" | "xl" | "2xl" | "3xl") default: "md" — Control height and typography scale
  - gutter-size ("3xs" | "2xs" | "xs" | "sm" | "md" | "lg" | "xl") — Override horizontal padding
  - icon-size ("sm" | "md" | "lg" | "xl" | "2xl") — Override icon size
  - href (string) — Link URL
  - to (string | RouteLocationRaw) — Router link destination (internal)
  - as (Component) — Link component override (e.g. RouterLink, NuxtLink)
  - external (boolean) — Force external-link behavior
  - optically-align ("start" | "end") — Apply optical negative margin alignment
  - block (boolean) default: false — Full width
  - pill (boolean) default: false — Fully rounded
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable link
  - disabled-tone ("relaxed") — Use relaxed visual tone for disabled state
  - @click ((event: MouseEvent) => void) — Emitted on click, unless disabled

### Overview
```vue
<ButtonLink color="primary" :pill="false" href="#">View API Keys <ArrowRight /></ButtonLink>
```

### Internal link
When a relative href or to is provided, ButtonLink uses the link component configured on PlexUIProvider, so your router handles navigation.
```vue
<ButtonLink href="/vue/components/button" color="primary">
  Internal link <ArrowRight />
</ButtonLink>
```

### External link
When linking to an external URL (or when external is set), &lt;a&gt; is used with target="_blank" and rel="noopener noreferrer" added automatically. If your link is not being automatically identified, you can force external link behavior with the external prop.
```vue
<ButtonLink href="#" color="primary">
  External link <ArrowUpRight />
</ButtonLink>
```

### Colors & variants
Stylize the link with color and variant props.
```vue
<div class="w-[calc(100%+48px)] -mx-6 overflow-x-auto [scrollbar-width:none] [&::-webkit-scrollbar]:hidden">
  <div class="pt-1 pb-6 px-8 w-fit min-w-full">
    <div class="grid gap-6 items-center justify-center" style="grid-template-columns: auto repeat(8, min-content)">
      <div />
      <div
        v-for="color in ['primary', 'secondary', 'danger', 'info', 'discovery', 'success', 'caution', 'warning']"
        :key="color"
        class="text-center text-sm mb-1 text-tertiary"
      >{{ color }}</div>
      <template v-for="variant in ['soft', 'solid', 'outline', 'ghost']" :key="variant">
        <div class="text-right text-sm mr-3 text-tertiary">{{ variant }}</div>
        <div
          v-for="color in ['primary', 'secondary', 'danger', 'info', 'discovery', 'success', 'caution', 'warning']"
          :key="color"
          class="text-center"
        >
          <ButtonLink href="#" size="lg" :color="color" :variant="variant" class="whitespace-nowrap">Button</ButtonLink>
        </div>
      </template>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
```

### Sizing & roundness
Sizing of the link is controlled with size and gutter-size. size sets the control height, and gutter-size sets the horizontal padding. Create fully rounded links with the pill prop. When pill is set, extra horizontal gutter is dynamically applied to the link.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2 justify-start items-start">
  <ButtonLink color="primary" :size="size" :pill="pill" href="#" class="whitespace-nowrap">
    View account <ArrowRight />
  </ButtonLink>
  <ButtonLink color="primary" :size="size" :pill="pill" href="#" class="whitespace-nowrap">
    View account
  </ButtonLink>
</div>
```

### Icons
Icon sizing is controlled by the icon-size prop, which makes it easy to add icons to the slot without additional props. size provides a default value for icon-size, but setting the prop forces the specified size.
```vue
<ButtonLink color="info" :size="size" :icon-size="iconSize" variant="soft" href="#" class="whitespace-nowrap">
  <Globe /> View website
</ButtonLink>
```

### Block
Create full-width links with the block prop.
```vue
<div class="w-[290px] text-center p-2 border border-dashed border-alpha/20 rounded-md">
  <ButtonLink :block="block" size="lg" href="#" class="whitespace-nowrap">Continue to dashboard</ButtonLink>
</div>
```

### Disabled
Use disabled to both visually and accessibly disable a link. The component renders as a span[role="link"], and click is not emitted.
```vue
<ButtonLink href="#" color="primary" :disabled="disabled" class="whitespace-nowrap">
  <Key /> View API Keys
</ButtonLink>
```

## Card
Card surface for composing login flows, sign-up forms, pricing tiles, payment methods, empty states, and stat showcases.
Import: import { Card, CardAction, CardContent, CardDescription, CardFooter, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@plexui/vue/components/Card"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/card
Props:
  - variant ("outline" | "solid" | "ghost") default: "outline" — Visual variant
  - size ("sm" | "md" | "lg") default: "md" — Padding scale
  - interactive (boolean) default: false — Adds hover and focus affordances. Automatic when href is set.
  - href (string) — Renders the Card as a link via the linkComponent of PlexUIProvider.
  - external (boolean) default: auto-detected — Forces target="_blank" and rel="noopener noreferrer" when href is set.
  - as (Component | "a") — Overrides the link component.
  - class (string) — Applied to the root element

### Overview
```vue
<Card style="max-width: 380px">
  <CardHeader>
    <CardTitle>Project activity</CardTitle>
    <CardDescription>
      Your team opened 12 issues and closed 8 pull requests over the last 7 days.
    </CardDescription>
  </CardHeader>
  <CardFooter>
    <Button color="primary" variant="solid" size="sm">View report</Button>
    <Button color="secondary" variant="outline" size="sm">Dismiss</Button>
  </CardFooter>
</Card>
```

### Login
A classic login form with a primary Log in CTA and an inline Sign up link below.
```vue
<Card style="max-width: 380px">
  <CardHeader>
    <CardTitle>Log in to your account</CardTitle>
    <CardDescription>Enter your email below to log in to your account.</CardDescription>
  </CardHeader>
  <CardContent>
    <div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 20px">
      <Field label="Email">
        <Input type="email" placeholder="you@example.com" />
      </Field>
      <div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 6px">
        <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; gap: 8px">
          <Label for="card-login-password">Password</Label>
          <TextLink href="#" primary>Forgot your password?</TextLink>
        </div>
        <Input id="card-login-password" type="password" />
      </div>
    </div>
  </CardContent>
  <CardFooter style="flex-direction: column; align-items: stretch; gap: 12px">
    <Button color="primary" variant="solid" block>Log in</Button>
    <!-- Description sits flush under a title by default; here it follows a button -->
    <CardDescription class="text-center" style="margin: 0">
      Don't have an account?
      <TextLink href="#" primary>Sign up</TextLink>
    </CardDescription>
  </CardFooter>
</Card>
```

### Create project
Right-aligned footer with Cancel / Submit. Use style="justify-content: flex-end" on CardFooter.
```vue
<Card style="max-width: 380px">
  <CardHeader>
    <CardTitle>Create project</CardTitle>
    <CardDescription>Deploy a new project in one-click with sensible defaults.</CardDescription>
  </CardHeader>
  <CardContent>
    <div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 20px">
      <Field label="Name">
        <Input placeholder="billing-service" />
      </Field>
      <Field label="Region" description="Where your functions and database will live.">
        <Input placeholder="us-east-1" />
      </Field>
    </div>
  </CardContent>
  <CardFooter style="justify-content: flex-end">
    <Button color="secondary" variant="outline" size="sm">Cancel</Button>
    <Button color="primary" variant="solid" size="sm">Deploy</Button>
  </CardFooter>
</Card>
```

### Payment Method
Full-fidelity payment form. Card Number + CVV share a 2fr/1fr row with a helper line; Month/Year use Select. Sections are divided by subtle hrs for Billing Address and Comments.
```vue
<Card style="max-width: 420px">
  <div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 16px">
    <CardHeader>
      <CardTitle>Payment Method</CardTitle>
      <CardDescription>All transactions are secure and encrypted</CardDescription>
    </CardHeader>

    <Field label="Name on Card">
      <Input placeholder="Aisha Cooper" />
    </Field>

    <Field label="Card Number" description="Enter your 16-digit card number">
      <Input placeholder="1234 5678 9012 3456" inputmode="numeric" />
    </Field>

    <div style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr 1fr; gap: 16px">
      <Field label="Month">
        <Select v-model:value="month" :options="monthOptions" placeholder="MM" />
      </Field>
      <Field label="Year">
        <Select v-model:value="year" :options="yearOptions" placeholder="YYYY" />
      </Field>
      <Field label="CVV">
        <Input placeholder="123" inputmode="numeric" />
      </Field>
    </div>

    <Separator />

    <CardHeader>
      <CardTitle>Billing Address</CardTitle>
      <CardDescription>The billing address associated with your payment method</CardDescription>
    </CardHeader>

    <Checkbox v-model:checked="sameAsShipping" label="Same as shipping address" />

    <Separator />

    <Field label="Comments">
      <Textarea placeholder="Add any additional comments" :rows="3" />
    </Field>

    <div style="display: flex; gap: 8px">
      <Button color="primary" variant="solid">Submit</Button>
      <Button color="secondary" variant="outline">Cancel</Button>
    </div>
  </div>
</Card>
```

### No team members (empty state)
Compose Card with EmptyMessage for a center-aligned empty state with icon badge, title, description, and primary action.
```vue
<Card style="max-width: 380px; padding: 24px">
  <EmptyMessage>
    <EmptyMessageIcon>
      <UserAdd />
    </EmptyMessageIcon>
    <EmptyMessageTitle>No team members</EmptyMessageTitle>
    <EmptyMessageDescription>
      Invite your team to collaborate on this project.
    </EmptyMessageDescription>
    <EmptyMessageActionRow>
      <Button color="primary" variant="solid" size="sm">Invite members</Button>
    </EmptyMessageActionRow>
  </EmptyMessage>
</Card>
```

### How did you hear about us?
A single-question survey using RadioGroup with direction="col" and a full-width submit button.
```vue
<Card style="max-width: 380px">
  <CardHeader>
    <CardTitle>How did you hear about us?</CardTitle>
    <CardDescription>Select the option that best describes how you heard about us.</CardDescription>
  </CardHeader>
  <CardContent>
    <RadioGroup v-model:value="source" direction="col">

    </RadioGroup>
  </CardContent>
  <CardFooter>
    <Button color="primary" variant="solid" block>Submit</Button>
  </CardFooter>
</Card>
```

### Processing your request
Loading state built with EmptyMessage and a LoadingIndicator as the visual anchor.
```vue
<Card style="max-width: 380px; padding: 24px">
  <EmptyMessage>
    <div style="margin-bottom: 12px; color: var(--color-text-tertiary)">
      <LoadingIndicator :size="28" />

  </EmptyMessage>
```
