
### Pricing
Mix variant="outline" and variant="solid" to emphasize a highlighted plan.
```vue
<div style="display: grid; gap: 16px; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(240px, 1fr)); max-width: 720px">
  <Card
    v-for="plan in [
      { name: 'Starter', price: '$0', highlighted: false, features: ['1 project', 'Community support', 'Plex UI core'] },
      { name: 'Pro', price: '$19', highlighted: true, features: ['Unlimited projects', 'Priority support', 'All Figma kits', 'Early components'] },
    ]"
    :key="plan.name"
    :variant="plan.highlighted ? 'solid' : 'outline'"
  >
    <CardHeader>
      <CardTitle>{{ plan.name }}</CardTitle>
      <CardDescription>
        <span style="font-size: 28px; font-weight: 600; color: var(--color-text)">{{ plan.price }}</span>
        <span style="margin-left: 4px">/ month</span>
      </CardDescription>
    </CardHeader>
    <CardContent>
      <ul style="list-style: none; padding: 0; margin: 0; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px">
        <li v-for="feature in plan.features" :key="feature" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px">
          <Check style="width: 16px; height: 16px; color: var(--color-text-success-soft)" />
          {{ feature }}
        </li>
      </ul>
    </CardContent>
    <CardFooter style="margin-top: auto; padding-top: 4px">
      <Button
        :color="plan.highlighted ? 'primary' : 'secondary'"
        :variant="plan.highlighted ? 'solid' : 'outline'"
        block
      >
        Choose {{ plan.name }}
      </Button>
    </CardFooter>
  </Card>
</div>
```

## Checkbox
Checkbox for binary on/off selection with solid, pill, and ghost variants, an indeterminate state, and rich labels with inline descriptions.
Import: import { Checkbox } from "@plexui/vue/components/Checkbox"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/checkbox
Props:
  - variant ("solid" | "ghost") default: "solid" — Visual style: solid (filled background) or ghost (checkmark only)
  - pill (boolean) default: false — Fully rounded indicator corners
  - checked / v-model:checked (boolean | "indeterminate") — Controlled state
  - default-checked (boolean | "indeterminate") — Initial state (uncontrolled)
  - label (string) — Accessible label rendered next to the checkbox
  - label slot (VNode[]) — Rich label content; takes precedence over the label prop
  - description (string) — Second line below the label, linked via aria-describedby (or the description slot)
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Prevent interaction
  - required (boolean) — Required for form submission
  - name (string) — Form field name
  - value (string) default: "on" — Value when submitted
  - orientation ("left" | "right") default: "left" — Checkbox position relative to the label
  - class (string) — CSS classes applied to the wrapper node
  - id (string) — The id of the checkbox element (generated when omitted)
  - @update:checked ((next: boolean | "indeterminate") => void) — Emitted with the next state when it changes (a click always emits a boolean)
  - @focus ((event: FocusEvent) => void) — Emitted when the checkbox gains focus
  - @blur ((event: FocusEvent) => void) — Emitted when the checkbox loses focus

### Overview
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-3">
  <Checkbox label="Same as billing address" :variant="variant" :pill="pill" :disabled="disabled" />
  <Checkbox label="Subscribe to newsletter" :variant="variant" :pill="pill" :disabled="disabled" default-checked />
</div>
```

### With Radio Group
Place Checkbox alongside RadioGroup controls to verify that indicator sizes, label baselines, and spacing align consistently in both horizontal and vertical layouts.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-6 w-full max-w-md">
  <Field label="Notification channel" description="Choose how you'd like to be notified." v-slot="{ id }">
    <RadioGroup :direction="direction" default-value="email" aria-label="Notification channel">
      <RadioGroupItem
        v-for="channel in [
          { value: 'email', label: 'Email', description: 'Messages delivered to your inbox.' },
          { value: 'sms', label: 'SMS', description: 'Text messages to your phone number.' },
          { value: 'push', label: 'Push', description: 'Real-time alerts on your device.' },
        ]"
        :key="channel.value"
        :value="channel.value"
      >
        <div v-if="description === 'true'" class="flex flex-col">
          <span class="font-medium">{{ channel.label }}</span>
          <span class="text-xs text-secondary mt-0.5">{{ channel.description }}</span>
        </div>
        <template v-else>{{ channel.label }}</template>
      </RadioGroupItem>
    </RadioGroup>
  </Field>
  <Field label="Topics" description="Select the topics you want to follow." v-slot="{ id }">
    <div :class="direction === 'row' ? 'flex flex-row gap-5' : 'flex flex-col gap-2.5'">
      <Checkbox
        v-for="topic in [
          { label: 'Product updates', description: 'New features and improvements.', checked: true },
          { label: 'Security alerts', description: 'Account activity and login notifications.', checked: true },
          { label: 'Community', description: 'Events, meetups, and discussions.', checked: false },
        ]"
        :key="topic.label"
        :default-checked="topic.checked"
      >
        <template #label>
          <div v-if="description === 'true'" class="flex flex-col">
            <span class="font-medium">{{ topic.label }}</span>
            <span class="text-xs text-secondary mt-0.5">{{ topic.description }}</span>
          </div>
          <template v-else>{{ topic.label }}</template>
        </template>
      </Checkbox>
    </div>
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Descriptions
Combine Field-level label and description with item-level descriptions for options that need additional context. Use a plain label without descriptions when the options are self-explanatory.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col max-w-sm w-full divide-y divide-alpha/15">
  <div class="pb-5">
    <Field label="Email notifications" description="Configure which emails you receive." v-slot="{ id }">
      <div class="flex flex-col gap-2.5">
        <Checkbox v-model:checked="settings.marketing">
          <template #label>
            <div class="flex flex-col">
              <span class="font-medium">Marketing</span>
              <span class="text-xs text-secondary mt-0.5">Receive emails about new products and features.</span>
            </div>
          </template>
        </Checkbox>
        <Checkbox v-model:checked="settings.security">
          <template #label>
            <div class="flex flex-col">
              <span class="font-medium">Security</span>
              <span class="text-xs text-secondary mt-0.5">Receive emails about account activity and security.</span>
            </div>
          </template>
        </Checkbox>
      </div>
    </Field>
  </div>
  <div class="pt-5">
    <Field label="Push notifications" v-slot="{ id }">
      <div class="flex flex-col gap-3">
        <Checkbox label="Everything" v-model:checked="settings.everything" />
        <Checkbox label="Direct messages and mentions" v-model:checked="settings.mentions" />
        <Checkbox label="Nothing" v-model:checked="settings.nothing" />
      </div>
    </Field>
  </div>
</div>
```

### Variants
The checkbox supports three visual styles: solid (default) with a filled background and standard radius, solid pill with fully rounded corners, and ghost, which renders only the checkmark without a border or background fill.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-wrap gap-x-12 gap-y-6">
  <div
    v-for="group in [
      { name: 'solid' },
      { name: 'solid pill', pill: true },
      { name: 'ghost', variant: 'ghost' },
    ]"
    :key="group.name"
    class="flex flex-col gap-3 items-start"
  >
    <span class="text-xs text-secondary font-mono mb-1">{{ group.name }}</span>
    <Checkbox label="Unchecked" :pill="group.pill" :variant="group.variant" :disabled="disabled" />
    <Checkbox label="Checked" :pill="group.pill" :variant="group.variant" :disabled="disabled" default-checked />
    <Checkbox label="Indeterminate" :pill="group.pill" :variant="group.variant" :disabled="disabled" checked="indeterminate" />
  </div>
</div>
```

### Pill
Set pill to render the checkbox indicator with fully rounded corners (border-radius: full) instead of the default square radius.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-3">
  <Checkbox label="Unchecked" :pill="pill" />
  <Checkbox label="Checked" :pill="pill" default-checked />
  <Checkbox label="Indeterminate" :pill="pill" checked="indeterminate" />
</div>
```

### Ghost
Use variant="ghost" to render the checkbox without a border or background. The checkmark uses the text color instead of white, making it suitable for subtle inline selections or task lists.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-3">
  <Checkbox label="Unchecked" variant="ghost" :disabled="disabled" />
  <Checkbox label="Checked" variant="ghost" :disabled="disabled" default-checked />
  <Checkbox label="Indeterminate" variant="ghost" :disabled="disabled" checked="indeterminate" />
</div>
```

### With description
Use description (or the description slot) for a second line under the label. It sits outside the label, so the checkbox keeps the label as its name and gets the description through aria-describedby. The description renders only next to a label.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-4 max-w-sm">
  <Checkbox
    label="Accept terms and conditions"
    description="By checking this box, you agree to our Terms of Service and Privacy Policy."
    default-checked
  />
  <Checkbox
    label="Enable notifications"
    description="You can enable or disable notifications at any time from settings."
  />
</div>
```

### Indeterminate
Use checked="indeterminate" to indicate a partially selected state. This is typically used for "select all" controls where only some child items are checked. Here checked starts as "indeterminate" and each click moves it to the next state.
```vue
<Checkbox
  :checked="checked"
  @update:checked="checked = checked === 'indeterminate' ? true : checked ? false : 'indeterminate'"
  label="Select all"
/>
```

### Disabled
When disabled is set, the checkbox is dimmed to 50% opacity and prevents interaction. It works the same across all variants: solid, pill, and ghost.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-wrap gap-x-12 gap-y-6">
  <div
    v-for="group in [
      { name: 'solid' },
      { name: 'solid pill', pill: true },
      { name: 'ghost', variant: 'ghost' },
    ]"
    :key="group.name"
    class="flex flex-col gap-3 items-start"
  >
    <span class="text-xs text-secondary font-mono mb-1">{{ group.name }}</span>
    <Checkbox label="Unchecked" :pill="group.pill" :variant="group.variant" disabled />
    <Checkbox label="Checked" :pill="group.pill" :variant="group.variant" disabled default-checked />
    <Checkbox label="Indeterminate" :pill="group.pill" :variant="group.variant" disabled checked="indeterminate" />
  </div>
</div>
```

### Label orientation
By default the checkbox renders to the left of the label. Set orientation="right" to place the checkbox after the label.
```vue
<Checkbox label="Accept terms and conditions" :orientation="orientation" />
```

### Without label
When no label is provided, the checkbox renders as a standalone control, so pass aria-label to give it an accessible name. All three visual states are shown: unchecked, checked, and indeterminate.
```vue
<div class="flex gap-4">
  <Checkbox aria-label="Accept terms" />
  <Checkbox aria-label="Subscribe" default-checked />
  <Checkbox aria-label="Select all" checked="indeterminate" />
</div>
```

### Group with select all
Combine a parent checkbox with child checkboxes to create a "select all" pattern. The parent shows an indeterminate state when only some children are checked. Each child binds v-model:checked to its item in items.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-3">
  <Checkbox
    label="Permissions"
    :checked="items.every((i) => i.checked) ? true : items.some((i) => i.checked) ? 'indeterminate' : false"
    @update:checked="(next) => items.forEach((i) => (i.checked = next))"
  />
  <div class="flex flex-col gap-3 pl-6">
    <Checkbox
      v-for="item in items"
      :key="item.id"
      :label="item.label"
      v-model:checked="item.checked"
    />
  </div>
</div>
```

### Group with description
Wrap a group of checkboxes in a Field for a shared label and description. This is a common pattern for preference lists and settings panels.
```vue
<div class="max-w-sm w-full">
  <Field label="Show on desktop" description="Select the items you want to display on the desktop." v-slot="{ id }">
    <div class="flex flex-col gap-3">
      <Checkbox v-for="item in items" :key="item.id" :label="item.label" v-model:checked="item.checked" />
    </div>
  </Field>
</div>
```

### With Field
Wrap checkboxes in a Field component for form layout with label, description, and error message support. The Field provides aria-describedby and aria-invalid linking automatically.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-4 max-w-sm w-full">
  <Field
    label="Terms"
    description="Please review and accept the terms before continuing."
    :error-message="showError ? 'You must accept the terms to continue.' : undefined"
    v-slot="{ id }"
  >
    <div class="flex flex-col gap-3 mt-1">
      <Checkbox label="I agree to the Terms of Service" v-model:checked="agreed" />
      <Checkbox label="Subscribe to product updates" />
    </div>
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Grouped sections
Use dividers to separate checkbox groups into logical sections. Each section can be wrapped in a Field with its own label and description.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col max-w-sm w-full divide-y divide-alpha/15">
  <div class="pb-5">
    <Field label="Email notifications" description="Configure which emails you receive." v-slot="{ id }">
      <div class="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <Checkbox v-model:checked="settings.emailMarketing">
          <template #label>
            <div class="flex flex-col">
              <span class="font-medium">Marketing</span>
              <span class="text-xs text-secondary mt-0.5">Receive emails about new products and features.</span>
            </div>
          </template>
        </Checkbox>
        <Checkbox v-model:checked="settings.emailSecurity">
          <template #label>
            <div class="flex flex-col">
              <span class="font-medium">Security</span>
              <span class="text-xs text-secondary mt-0.5">Receive emails about account activity and security.</span>
            </div>
          </template>
        </Checkbox>
      </div>
    </Field>
  </div>
  <div class="pt-5">
    <Field label="Push notifications" description="Configure which push notifications you receive." v-slot="{ id }">
      <div class="flex flex-col gap-3">
        <Checkbox label="Everything" v-model:checked="settings.pushEverything" />
        <Checkbox label="Direct messages and mentions" v-model:checked="settings.pushMentions" />
        <Checkbox label="Nothing" v-model:checked="settings.pushNothing" />
      </div>
    </Field>
  </div>
</div>
```

## Code Block
Code block with syntax highlighting, a copy button, and collapsible long snippets with configurable expanded height limits.
Import: import { CodeBlock } from "@plexui/vue/components/CodeBlock"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/code-block
Props:
  - default slot (string) — Code content (text only; interpolate a string to keep its whitespace)
  - code (string) — Code content, used when the default slot is empty
  - language (string) — Prism language name
  - class (string) — Additional wrapper class
  CollapsibleCodeBlock:
    - default slot / code (string) — Code content
    - language (string) — Prism language name
    - default-expanded (boolean) default: false — Start expanded
    - collapsed-height (number) default: 140 — Height when collapsed (px)
    - max-expanded-height (number) — Max height when expanded (px)
    - copyable (boolean) default: true — Show copy button

### Overview
The code is the text of the default slot. Interpolate a string ({"{{ code }}"}) so its whitespace is kept, or pass it with the code prop.
```vue
<CodeBlock language="javascript">{{ codeSnippet }}</CodeBlock>
```

### Collapsible
Use CollapsibleCodeBlock to display code in a compact view with gradient overlay and an expand action.
```vue
<CollapsibleCodeBlock language="tsx">{{ code }}</CollapsibleCodeBlock>
```

### Expanded by default
```vue
<CollapsibleCodeBlock language="tsx" default-expanded>{{ code }}</CollapsibleCodeBlock>
```

### Custom collapsed height
```vue
<CollapsibleCodeBlock language="typescript" :collapsed-height="60">{{ code }}</CollapsibleCodeBlock>
```

### Max expanded height
```vue
<CollapsibleCodeBlock language="typescript" default-expanded :max-expanded-height="200">{{ code }}</CollapsibleCodeBlock>
```

### Without copy button
```vue
<CollapsibleCodeBlock language="python" default-expanded :copyable="false">{{ code }}</CollapsibleCodeBlock>
```

### Supported languages
A pragmatic set of Prism grammars are pre-registered out of the box.
```vue
<CodeBlock language="javascript">{{ code }}</CodeBlock>
```

## Copy Tooltip
A copy-to-clipboard tooltip that wraps any trigger element and confirms when the value has been copied.
Import: import { CopyTooltip, TooltipTriggerDecorator } from "@plexui/vue/components/Tooltip"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/copy-tooltip
Props:
  - default slot (VNode) — Trigger element (for example TooltipTriggerDecorator)
  - copyValue (string) — Required. Content to copy to clipboard
  - openDelay (number) default: 150 — Tooltip open delay in milliseconds
  - align ("start" | "center" | "end") default: "center" — Preferred tooltip alignment against the trigger
  - alignOffset (number) default: 0 — Offset from start/end alignment in pixels
  - side ("top" | "right" | "bottom" | "left") default: "top" — Preferred side of the trigger
  - sideOffset (number) default: 5 — Distance from trigger in pixels
  - @copy ((copied: boolean) => void) — Emitted once the clipboard write settles, with whether it succeeded. The tooltip says Copied! only after a successful write

### Overview
```vue
<CopyTooltip
  copy-value="Very cool content to copy"
  :open-delay="openDelay"
  :side-offset="sideOffset"
  :align-offset="alignOffset"
  :side="side"
  :align="align"
>
  <TooltipTriggerDecorator>sess_12345abcdefg</TooltipTriggerDecorator>
</CopyTooltip>
```

## Date Input
Masked text input for typing dates and times, with automatic formatting, min and max limits, and validation.
Import: import { DateInput } from "@plexui/vue/components/DateInput"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/date-input
Props:
  - value / v-model:value (DateTime | null) — Selected date (Luxon DateTime)
  - @update:value ((date: DateTime | null) => void) — Emitted when a complete valid date is entered or the field is cleared
  - @change ((date: DateTime | null) => void) — Same payload as update:value, named after React's onChange
  - mode (MaskitoDateMode) default: "dd/mm/yyyy" — Date format mode: dd/mm/yyyy, mm/dd/yyyy, yyyy/mm/dd, etc.
  - separator (string) default: "." — Character between date parts
  - time-mode (MaskitoTimeMode) — Enable time input: HH:MM, HH:MM:SS, etc.
  - date-time-separator (string) default: ", " — Separator between date and time parts
  - time-step (number) default: 0 — Arrow-key increment step for time segments
  - min (DateTime) — Earliest allowed date
  - max (DateTime) — Latest allowed date
  - clearable (boolean) default: false — Show clear button when input has a value
  - size (ControlSize) default: "md" — Input size (3xs to 3xl)
  - variant ("outline" | "soft") default: "outline" — Visual style
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable input
  - invalid (boolean) default: false — Mark as invalid
  - placeholder (string) — Generated from the format if not set

### Overview
Bind a Luxon DateTime (or null) with v-model:value. It updates once a complete, valid date is typed, and becomes null when the field is emptied or cleared; @change carries the same value.
```vue
<div class="w-[200px]">
  <DateInput
    v-model:value="date"
    :size="size"
    :variant="variant"
    :pill="pill"
    :clearable="clearable"
  />
</div>
```

### With time
Pass time-mode="HH:MM" to enable date + time input in one field.
```vue
<div class="w-[260px]">
  <DateInput v-model:value="date" time-mode="HH:MM" clearable />
</div>
```

### With seconds
Use time-mode="HH:MM:SS" for second-level precision.
```vue
<div class="w-[300px]">
  <DateInput v-model:value="date" time-mode="HH:MM:SS" clearable />
</div>
```

### Limits
Use min and max to restrict the date range. The mask prevents entering values outside the allowed range. today is DateTime.local().
```vue
<div class="w-[200px]">
  <DateInput
    v-model:value="date"
    :min="today.minus({ days: 30 }).startOf('day')"
    :max="today.plus({ days: 30 }).endOf('day')"
    clearable
  />
</div>
```

### US format
Set mode="mm/dd/yyyy" and separator="/" for US-style date formatting.
```vue
<div class="w-[200px]">
  <DateInput v-model:value="date" mode="mm/dd/yyyy" separator="/" clearable />
</div>
```

### ISO format
Set mode="yyyy/mm/dd" and separator="-" for ISO 8601 formatting.
```vue
<div class="w-[200px]">
  <DateInput v-model:value="date" mode="yyyy/mm/dd" separator="-" clearable />
</div>
```

### With Field
Wrap with Field to add a label, description, and automatic accessibility attributes.
```vue
<div class="w-[200px]">
  <Field label="Date of birth" description="Enter your date of birth">
    <DateInput v-model:value="date" clearable />
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Validation
Pass error-message to Field to display a validation error.
```vue
<div class="w-[200px]">
  <Field label="Date of birth" :error-message="!date ? 'Date is required' : undefined">
    <DateInput v-model:value="date" clearable />
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Disabled
```vue
<div class="w-[200px]">
  <DateInput v-model:value="date" disabled />
</div>
```

## Date Picker
Date picker with an interactive calendar, time picker, year and month dropdowns, min/max limits, and date-of-birth presets.
Import: import { DatePicker } from "@plexui/vue/components/DatePicker"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/date-picker
Props:
  - v-model:value (DateTime | null) — Selected date (luxon)
  - @change ((date: DateTime | null) => void) — Called when date changes
  - min (DateTime) — Minimum selectable date
  - max (DateTime) — Maximum selectable date
  - caption-layout ("buttons" | "dropdown") default: "buttons" — Calendar header mode. Use dropdown for date of birth
  - show-time (boolean) default: false — Show time input below calendar
  - year-range ([number, number]) — Min/max years for dropdown caption
  - size (ControlSize) — Trigger size (3xs to 3xl)
  - pill (boolean) — Rounded trigger
  - clearable (boolean) — Show clear button
  - trigger-show-icon (boolean) default: true — Show calendar icon
  - trigger-date-format (string) default: "MM/dd/yy" — Date format in trigger. Auto-includes time when show-time is enabled
  - placeholder (string) — Trigger placeholder
  - disabled (boolean) — Disable picker

### Overview
Bind the date (a luxon DateTime, or null) with v-model:value. The @change event carries the same value, like React's onChange.
```vue
<DatePicker
  id="date-picker-base"
  trigger-date-format="MM/dd/yy"
  size="lg"
  pill
  clearable
  v-model:value="selectedDate"
/>
```

### Limits
Use min and max to limit selectable dates to a specific timeframe. Here minDate is {"today.minus({ days: 30 }).startOf('day')"} and maxDate is {"today.plus({ days: 30 }).endOf('day')"}.
```vue
<DatePicker
  id="date-picker-limits"
  trigger-date-format="MM/dd/yy"
  size="lg"
  pill
  trigger-show-icon
  v-model:value="selectedDate"
  :min="minDate"
  :max="maxDate"
/>
```

### Date of birth
Set caption-layout="dropdown" to add month and year dropdowns inline with the arrow navigation. Ideal for selecting a date of birth where navigating month-by-month would be impractical.
```vue
<DatePicker
  id="date-picker-dob"
  trigger-date-format="MMMM d, yyyy"
  size="lg"
  pill
  clearable
  placeholder="Select your birthday"
  caption-layout="dropdown"
  v-model:value="selectedDate"
  :max="DateTime.local()"
/>
```

### Time picker
Enable show-time to add hour and minute inputs below the calendar. The popover stays open after selecting a date so the user can adjust the time. Use arrow keys to increment or decrement values.
```vue
<DatePicker
  id="date-picker-time"
  trigger-date-format="MM/dd/yy HH:mm"
  size="lg"
  pill
  clearable
  placeholder="Select date & time"
  show-time
  v-model:value="selectedDate"
/>
```

### Dropdown + time
Combine caption-layout="dropdown" with show-time for quick month/year jumps and time selection in a single picker, useful for scheduling events at a specific date and time.
```vue
<DatePicker
  id="date-picker-datetime-dropdown"
  trigger-date-format="MMM d, yyyy HH:mm"
  size="lg"
  pill
  clearable
  placeholder="Schedule event"
  caption-layout="dropdown"
  show-time
  :year-range="[2020, 2030]"
  v-model:value="selectedDate"
/>
```

### Two fields
Compose DatePicker and Input side by side for maximum flexibility in form layouts. Each field operates independently while sharing the same DateTime value. Here formatTime masks the typed time as HH:MM, applyTime moves the date to a complete typed time, and withTime gives a newly picked date the typed time.
```vue
<div style="display: flex; gap: 8px; align-items: center">
  <DatePicker
    id="date-picker-two-field-date"
    trigger-date-format="MMM d, yyyy"
    size="lg"
    pill
    caption-layout="dropdown"
    :trigger-show-icon="false"
    dropdown-icon-type="none"
    placeholder="Select date"
    :value="selectedDate"
    @update:value="selectedDate = withTime($event, time)"
  />
  <Input
    id="date-picker-two-field-time"
    size="lg"
    pill
    :value="time"
    @update:value="time = formatTime($event); selectedDate = applyTime(selectedDate, time)"
    placeholder="HH:MM"
    maxlength="5"
    style="width: 72px"
  />
</div>
```

### Date input
For typing a date directly instead of picking it, use DateInput, a masked input that auto-formats as you type.
```vue
<div class="w-[200px]">
  <DateInput v-model:value="selectedDate" mode="mm/dd/yyyy" separator="/" aria-label="Date" />
</div>
```

### DateTime input
Pass time-mode="HH:MM" to DateInput to type the date and time in one field.
```vue
<div class="w-[260px]">
  <DateInput
    v-model:value="selectedDate"
    mode="mm/dd/yyyy"
    separator="/"
    time-mode="HH:MM"
    aria-label="Date and time"
  />
</div>
```

### Without Icon
Remove the calendar icon from the trigger by setting trigger-show-icon to false.
```vue
<DatePicker
  id="date-picker-no-icon"
  trigger-date-format="MM/dd/yy"
  size="lg"
  :trigger-show-icon="false"
  placeholder="Choose a date"
  v-model:value="selectedDate"
/>
```

### Disabled
Disable the picker to prevent user interaction.
```vue
<DatePicker
  id="date-picker-disabled"
  trigger-date-format="MM/dd/yy"
  size="lg"
  disabled
  v-model:value="selectedDate"
/>
```

## Date Range Picker
Date range picker with an interactive calendar, min/max limits, preset shortcuts, and a month stepper for selecting start and end dates.
Import: import { DateRangePicker } from "@plexui/vue/components/DateRangePicker"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/date-range-picker
Props:
  - v-model:value (DateRange | null) — Selected range ([start, end], inclusive)
  - @change ((next: DateRange | null, shortcut?: DateRangeShortcut) => void) — Called when range changes
  - min (DateTime) — Earliest selectable date
  - max (DateTime) — Latest selectable date
  - max-range-days (number) — Max length of range in days (inclusive)
  - shortcuts (DateRangeShortcut[]) — Quick selection shortcuts
  - side ("top" | "right" | "bottom" | "left") default: "bottom" — Preferred side for popover
  - side-offset (number) default: 8 — Distance from trigger in pixels
  - align ("start" | "center" | "end") default: "center" — Preferred popover alignment
  - align-offset (number) default: align === "center" ? 0 : -5 — Offset from start/end alignment in pixels
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable picker interactions
  - placeholder (string) default: "Select date range..." — Placeholder in trigger when value is empty
  - variant ("soft" | "outline" | "ghost") default: "outline" — Trigger style variant
  - pill (boolean) default: true — Rounded trigger shape
  - size ("3xs" | "2xs" | "xs" | "sm" | "md" | "lg" | "xl" | "2xl" | "3xl") default: "md" — Trigger size scale
  - dropdown-icon-type ("chevronDown" | "dropdown" | "none") default: "dropdown" — Icon shown at trigger end
  - trigger-class-name (string) — Custom class for trigger
  - trigger-show-icon (boolean) default: true — Show calendar icon at trigger start
  - trigger-stepper-unit ("month") — Enable month stepper mode in trigger
  - trigger-date-format (string) default: "MM/dd/yy" — Date formatting in trigger text
  - clearable (boolean) default: false — Show clear button in trigger
  - block (boolean) default: false — Stretch trigger to full available width

### Overview
Bind the range ([start, end] luxon DateTimes, or null) with v-model:value. The @change event carries the same range and, when a shortcut was picked, that shortcut as its second argument, like React's onChange(range, shortcut).
```vue
<div style="width: fit-content">
  <DateRangePicker
    size="lg"
    pill
    clearable
    v-model:value="value"
    :shortcuts="dayShortcuts"
  />
</div>
```

### Limits
Use min and max to limit the selectable range. Use max-range-days to limit the range length. Here minDate is 30 days ago (start of day) and maxDate is the end of today.
```vue
<div style="width: fit-content">
  <DateRangePicker
    size="lg"
    pill
    trigger-show-icon
    v-model:value="value"
    :min="minDate"
    :max="maxDate"
  />
</div>
```

### Shortcuts
Use the shortcuts array to provide quick date range options. When a shortcut is selected, its label is shown in the trigger. Each shortcut has a label and a getDateRange() function; dayShortcuts here holds Today, Yesterday, Last 7 days and Last 30 days.
```vue
<div style="width: fit-content">
  <DateRangePicker
    size="lg"
    pill
    clearable
    v-model:value="value"
    :shortcuts="dayShortcuts"
  />
</div>
```

### Month stepper
Enable a stepper in the trigger with trigger-stepper-unit="month". When the selected range matches the unit, the trigger shows the stepper.
```vue
<div style="width: fit-content">
  <DateRangePicker
    :size="size"
    clearable
    :pill="pill"
    trigger-stepper-unit="month"
    v-model:value="value"
    :max="maxDate"
  />
</div>
```

## Dialog
Modal dialog for confirmations, forms, and destructive alerts, with a focus trap, escape handling, and an overlay scrim.
Import: import { Dialog, DialogBody, DialogClose, DialogContent, DialogFooter, DialogHeader, DialogTitle, DialogTrigger } from "@plexui/vue/components/Dialog"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/dialog
Props:
  - open / v-model:open (boolean) — Controlled open state; uncontrolled when omitted
  - @update:open ((open: boolean) => void) — Fires when the dialog opens or closes
  DialogContent:
    - width (number | string) default: 480 — Panel width
    - show-close (boolean) default: true — Show close button in top-right corner
    - class (string) — Additional CSS class on the panel
    - placement ("center" | "top" | "bottom" | "start" | "end" | "fullscreen") default: "center" — Where the panel sits: centered, near the top, a bottom sheet, a side panel, or the whole screen
    - mobile-placement ("center" | "bottom" | "fullscreen") — Placement at 640px and below; defaults to placement
    - @open-auto-focus ((event: Event) => void) — Called when focus moves into the dialog on open; call event.preventDefault() to place it yourself, for example on the panel instead of the first field
    - @close-auto-focus ((event: Event) => void) — Called when focus returns on close; call event.preventDefault() to keep it elsewhere

### Overview
```vue
<Dialog v-model:open="open">
  <DialogTrigger>
    <Button color="secondary" variant="outline">Open dialog</Button>
  </DialogTrigger>
  <DialogContent>
    <DialogHeader>
      <DialogTitle>Unsaved changes</DialogTitle>
    </DialogHeader>
    <p class="text-sm text-secondary">You have unsaved changes that will be lost if you close this page. Do you want to save before leaving?</p>
    <DialogFooter>
      <DialogClose>
        <Button color="primary" variant="soft">Cancel</Button>
      </DialogClose>
      <Button color="primary" @click="open = false">Save changes</Button>
    </DialogFooter>
  </DialogContent>
</Dialog>
```

### Destructive confirmation
Use color="danger" on the action button for destructive operations.
```vue
<Dialog v-model:open="open">
  <DialogTrigger>
    <Button color="danger" variant="outline">Delete</Button>
  </DialogTrigger>
  <DialogContent>
    <DialogHeader>
      <DialogTitle>Delete assistant?</DialogTitle>
    </DialogHeader>
    <p class="text-sm text-secondary">
      This change will affect all API integrations using
      <span class="font-medium text-[var(--color-text)]">asst_NAM5K9a7loKZay9W8gtQVQ3H</span>.
      It cannot be undone.
    </p>
    <DialogFooter>
      <DialogClose>
        <Button color="secondary" variant="soft">Cancel</Button>
      </DialogClose>
      <Button color="danger" @click="open = false">Delete</Button>
    </DialogFooter>
  </DialogContent>
</Dialog>
```

### Scrolling body
Put long content in DialogBody: only the body scrolls, while the header and footer stay in view. Without it the whole panel scrolls.
```vue
<Dialog v-model:open="open">
  <DialogTrigger>
    <Button color="secondary" variant="outline">Review terms</Button>
  </DialogTrigger>
  <DialogContent>
    <DialogHeader>
      <DialogTitle>Terms of service</DialogTitle>
      <DialogDescription>Please read them before you continue.</DialogDescription>
    </DialogHeader>
    <DialogBody>
      <div class="flex flex-col gap-4 text-sm text-secondary">
        <p v-for="(term, index) in [...terms, ...terms]" :key="index">{{ term }}</p>
      </div>
    </DialogBody>
    <DialogFooter>
      <DialogClose>
        <Button color="secondary" variant="soft">Decline</Button>
      </DialogClose>
      <Button color="primary" @click="open = false">Accept</Button>
    </DialogFooter>
  </DialogContent>
</Dialog>
```

### Placement
Use placement to move the panel: top for search and command lists, bottom for a sheet along the bottom edge, start and end for a full-height side panel, fullscreen for the whole screen. mobile-placement applies at 640px and below, so a centered dialog can become a bottom sheet on phones: {''}.
```vue
<Dialog v-model:open="open">
  <DialogTrigger>
    <Button color="secondary" variant="outline">Open filters</Button>
  </DialogTrigger>
  <DialogContent :placement="placement" :width="placement === 'start' || placement === 'end' ? 360 : 480">
    <DialogHeader>
      <DialogTitle>Filters</DialogTitle>
      <DialogDescription>Narrow the list down.</DialogDescription>
    </DialogHeader>
    <DialogBody>
      <div class="flex flex-col gap-4">
        <Field label="Status"><Input placeholder="Any status" /></Field>
        <Field label="Owner"><Input placeholder="Anyone" /></Field>
        <Field label="Created after"><Input placeholder="YYYY-MM-DD" /></Field>
      </div>
    </DialogBody>
    <DialogFooter>
      <DialogClose>
        <Button color="secondary" variant="soft">Clear filters</Button>
      </DialogClose>
      <Button color="primary" @click="open = false">Done</Button>
    </DialogFooter>
  </DialogContent>
</Dialog>
```

## Empty Message
Empty state for telling users there's nothing to show, with error, warning, no-results, and onboarding compositions.
Import: import { EmptyMessage, EmptyMessageActionRow, EmptyMessageDescription, EmptyMessageIcon, EmptyMessageTitle } from "@plexui/vue/components/EmptyMessage"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/empty-message
Props:
  - fill ("static" | "absolute" | "none") default: "static" — How the container fills available space; use "none" for transparent (e.g. in search results)
  - size ("sm" | "md") default: "md" — sm is the compact message for a panel, a card section or an empty list
  - align ("center" | "start") default: "center" — start left-aligns the message, for an empty state under a section heading
  EmptyMessageIcon:
    - color ("secondary" | "danger" | "warning") default: "secondary" — Semantic color
    - size ("sm" | "md") default: "md" — Icon badge size
  EmptyMessageTitle:
    - color ("secondary" | "danger" | "warning") default: "secondary" — Semantic color
  EmptyMessageActionRow:
    - class (string) — Additional classes for the action row container

### Composition
```vue
<div
  class="flex w-full items-center justify-center"
  :style="fill === 'absolute' ? { position: 'relative', minHeight: '200px' } : undefined"
>
  <EmptyMessage :fill="fill">
    <EmptyMessageIcon v-if="icon === 'on'" :color="color" :size="size">
      <Explore />
    </EmptyMessageIcon>
    <EmptyMessageTitle v-if="content !== 'description'" :color="color">No projects yet</EmptyMessageTitle>
    <EmptyMessageDescription v-if="content !== 'title'">
      Create your first project to get started
    </EmptyMessageDescription>
    <EmptyMessageActionRow>
      <Button color="primary" :size="size === 'sm' ? 'md' : 'lg'">
        <Plus class="mr-[-2px]" />
        New project
      </Button>
    </EmptyMessageActionRow>
  </EmptyMessage>
</div>
```

### Error message
The color property enables you to display clearer intent with the message, such as showing an error state.
```vue
<EmptyMessage>
  <EmptyMessageIcon color="danger">
    <Mic />
  </EmptyMessageIcon>
  <EmptyMessageTitle color="danger">
    Enable microphone access in your browser's settings.
  </EmptyMessageTitle>
</EmptyMessage>
```

### Warning message
```vue
<EmptyMessage>
  <EmptyMessageIcon color="warning">
    <Sparkle />
  </EmptyMessageIcon>
  <EmptyMessageTitle>o1-preview is in beta</EmptyMessageTitle>
  <EmptyMessageDescription>
    System instructions and model configuration are not available yet. Responses may take
    longer.
  </EmptyMessageDescription>
  <EmptyMessageActionRow class="text-sm">
    <TextLink href="/">
      Learn more
      <ArrowUpRight />
    </TextLink>
  </EmptyMessageActionRow>
</EmptyMessage>
```

### No results
The parts of EmptyMessage are both composable and optional. This example omits EmptyMessageTitle, using only an icon and description for a simpler message style.
```vue
<div class="w-full">
  <EmptyMessage fill="none">
    <EmptyMessageIcon size="sm">
      <Search />
    </EmptyMessageIcon>
    <EmptyMessageDescription>
      No icons found matching <span class="font-semibold">"pizza"</span>
    </EmptyMessageDescription>
  </EmptyMessage>
</div>
```

### Compact
Use size="sm" for an empty panel, card section or list: a smaller title, tighter spacing and no minimum width. Pair it with fill="none" when the container sets the height.
```vue
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Card>
    <CardHeader>
      <CardTitle>Members</CardTitle>
    </CardHeader>
    <CardContent>
      <EmptyMessage size="sm" fill="none">
        <EmptyMessageIcon size="sm"><Group /></EmptyMessageIcon>
        <EmptyMessageTitle>No members yet</EmptyMessageTitle>
        <EmptyMessageDescription>Invite people to work on this project.</EmptyMessageDescription>
        <EmptyMessageActionRow>
          <Button color="secondary" variant="soft" size="sm">Invite</Button>
        </EmptyMessageActionRow>
      </EmptyMessage>
    </CardContent>
  </Card>
</div>
```

### Left aligned
Use align="start" for an empty state that sits under a section heading instead of filling a surface: a single line such as "No tags added" reads with the heading above it.
```vue
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Card>
    <CardHeader>
      <CardTitle>Tags</CardTitle>
    </CardHeader>
    <CardContent>
      <EmptyMessage size="sm" align="start" fill="none">
        <EmptyMessageDescription>No tags added.</EmptyMessageDescription>
      </EmptyMessage>
    </CardContent>
  </Card>
</div>
```

## Field
Form field wrapper with a label, helper text, an error message, and automatic accessibility linking to its control.
Import: import { Field } from "@plexui/vue/components/Field"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/field
Props:
  - label (string) — Label text for the field
  - label slot (VNode[]) — Rich label content; takes precedence over the label prop
  - description (string) — Helper text below the label; linked via aria-describedby (or the description slot)
  - error-message (string) — Error message below the control; sets aria-invalid on the child (or the errorMessage slot)
  - label-action (string) — Content at the end of the label row, such as a link that acts on the field (or the labelAction slot)
  - size ("3xs" | "2xs" | "xs" | "sm" | "md" | "lg" | "xl" | "2xl" | "3xl") default: child's size, else "md" — Label font size; defaults to the child control's size
  - required (boolean) default: false — Shows a required asterisk after the label (visual only)
  - orientation ("vertical" | "horizontal") default: "vertical" — Layout direction of label and control
  - optically-align ("start" | "end") — Applies optical alignment to the child control via negative margin using its gutter
  - id (string) — Override the generated id used for the label's for and aria linking
  - class (string) — CSS classes applied to the root element
  - default slot ((fieldProps) => VNode[]) — Form control; its single root element receives id, aria-describedby and aria-invalid (plus aria-labelledby unless it has its own name) unless the slot reads fieldProps, which include aria-labelledby

### Overview
Field wraps a form control with a label, optional description, and error message. It automatically generates the label's for, aria-describedby, and aria-invalid linking between the label, description, error, and child control.
```vue
<div :class="horizontal ? 'w-full max-w-[480px]' : 'w-full max-w-[320px]'">
  <Field
    label="Email"
    :size="size"
    :description="description ? 'We\'ll never share your email with anyone else.' : undefined"
    :error-message="errorMessage ? 'Please enter a valid email address.' : undefined"
    :required="required"
    :orientation="horizontal ? 'horizontal' : 'vertical'"
  >
    <Input placeholder="Enter email..." :size="size" :pill="pill" />
  </Field>
</div>
```

### With description
Use description to display helper text below the label. The description is automatically linked to the control via aria-describedby.
```vue
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Field label="Email" description="We'll never share your email with anyone else.">
    <Input placeholder="Enter email..." />
  </Field>
</div>
```

### With error message
Use error-message to display a validation error. When provided, the child control automatically receives aria-invalid="true". The error is rendered using the FieldError component with role="alert" for accessibility.
```vue
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Field
    label="Email"
    :error-message="errorMessage ? 'Please enter a valid email address' : undefined"
  >
    <Input placeholder="Enter email..." />
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Required
Use required to show a visual asterisk after the label. This is purely visual and does not add the required HTML attribute: pass required directly to the child control for form validation.
```vue
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Field label="Email" :required="required">
    <Input placeholder="Enter email..." />
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Sizing
The label and description scale with the child control: Field reads the control's size, so {''} gets a lg label without repeating it. Pass size to Field to set the label size yourself, or when the slot reads the field props.
```vue
<div :class="horizontal ? 'w-full max-w-[480px]' : 'w-full max-w-[320px]'">
  <Field
    label="Email"
    :size="size"
    :description="description ? 'We\'ll never share your email with anyone else.' : undefined"
    :error-message="errorMessage ? 'Please enter a valid email address.' : undefined"
    :required="required"
    :orientation="horizontal ? 'horizontal' : 'vertical'"
  >
    <Input placeholder="Enter email..." :size="size" :pill="pill" />
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Horizontal layout
Use orientation="horizontal" to render the label beside the control instead of above it.
```vue
<div class="w-full max-w-[480px] flex flex-col gap-4">
  <Field label="First name" :orientation="orientation">
    <Input placeholder="Jane" />
  </Field>
  <Field label="Last name" :orientation="orientation">
    <Input placeholder="Doe" />
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Textarea
```vue
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Field label="Bio" description="Write a short bio for your profile.">
    <Textarea placeholder="Tell us about yourself..." :rows="3" />
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Select
A Select trigger is not a native form control, so the label's for cannot name it. Field points the trigger's aria-labelledby at the label, and Select adds its value: a screen reader hears "Role, Admin". DatePicker works the same way. A control with its own aria-label, aria-labelledby or label keeps its name; a slot that reads the field props gets aria-labelledby among them to bind.
```vue
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Field label="Role" description="Select your primary role.">
    <Select
      placeholder="Select a role..."
      v-model:value="role"
      :options="[
        { label: 'Admin', value: 'admin' },
        { label: 'Editor', value: 'editor' },
        { label: 'Viewer', value: 'viewer' },
      ]"
    />
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Label action
Use the labelAction slot (or the label-action string prop) for a link or a small control that acts on the field, at the end of the label row. It takes the label's font size.
```vue
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Field label="Password">
    <template #labelAction>
      <TextLink href="#reset-password">Forgot password?</TextLink>
    </template>
    <Input type="password" placeholder="Enter password..." />
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Slider
Field works with any form control. Use the description to display the current slider value.
```vue
<div class="w-[400px]">
  <Field
    label="Price Range"
    :description="'Set your budget range ($' + price[0].toLocaleString() + ' – ' + price[1].toLocaleString() + ').'"
  >
    <Slider range v-model:value="price" :min="0" :max="1000" :step="10" prefix-unit="$" />
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Fieldset
Group related fields under a heading and description to create a logical fieldset.
```vue
<div class="w-[400px]">
  <div class="mb-3">
    <h3 class="text-xs font-medium text-secondary uppercase tracking-wider">Address Information</h3>
    <p class="text-xs text-tertiary mt-0.5">We need your address to deliver your order.</p>
  </div>
  <div class="flex flex-col gap-4">
    <Field label="Street Address">
      <Input placeholder="123 Main St" />
    </Field>
    <div class="flex gap-3">
      <Field label="City" class="flex-1">
        <Input placeholder="New York" />
      </Field>
      <Field label="Postal Code" class="flex-1">
        <Input placeholder="90502" />
      </Field>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
```

### Checkbox
Wrap a group of checkboxes in a Field for a shared label and description. The slot takes the field props (v-slot="{'{ id }'}"), so Field renders the group as it is instead of wiring the props onto it.
```vue
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Field
    label="Notifications"
    description="Choose which notifications you'd like to receive."
    v-slot="{ id }"
  >
    <div class="flex flex-col gap-3 mt-1">
      <Checkbox label="Email notifications" />
      <Checkbox label="Push notifications" />
      <Checkbox label="SMS notifications" />
    </div>
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Radio
Use Field with RadioGroup for labeled radio selections.
```vue
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Field label="Subscription plan" description="Select your preferred plan." v-slot="{ id }">
    <RadioGroup aria-label="Subscription plan" v-model:value="plan" direction="col">
      <RadioGroupItem value="free">Free</RadioGroupItem>
      <RadioGroupItem value="pro">Pro, $9/mo</RadioGroupItem>
      <RadioGroupItem value="enterprise">Enterprise, $29/mo</RadioGroupItem>
    </RadioGroup>
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Switch
Pair a Field with a Switch using orientation="horizontal" for inline toggle settings.
```vue
<div class="self-stretch mx-auto !max-w-[480px]">
  <Field
    label="Marketing emails"
    orientation="horizontal"
    class="justify-between [--field-horizontal-label-width:auto] [--field-horizontal-control-width:auto] [--field-label-horizontal-offset:0px]"
    v-slot="fieldProps"
  >
    <Switch :id="fieldProps.id" v-model:checked="enabled" />
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Field group
Stack Field components with a divider to group related notification settings.
```vue
<div class="w-[400px] divide-y divide-alpha/15">
  <div class="pb-6">
    <Field
      label="Responses"
      description="Get notified when the assistant responds to requests that take time, like research or image generation."
      v-slot="{ id }"
    >
      <div class="flex flex-col gap-3 mt-1">
        <Checkbox label="Push notifications" default-checked />
      </div>
    </Field>
  </div>
  <div class="py-6">
    <Field
      label="Tasks"
      description="Get notified when tasks you've created have updates."
      v-slot="{ id }"
    >
      <div class="flex flex-col gap-3 mt-1">
        <Checkbox label="Push notifications" />
        <Checkbox label="Email notifications" />
      </div>
    </Field>
  </div>
</div>
```

### Validation and Errors
Combine required and error-message for client-side validation feedback. Toggle errors on and off to see the visual transition.
```vue
<div class="w-[320px] flex flex-col gap-4">
  <Field label="Name" required :error-message="showErrors && !name ? 'Name is required' : undefined">
    <Input placeholder="Enter your name" v-model:value="name" />
  </Field>
  <Field
    label="Email"
    required
    :error-message="showErrors && !email.includes('@') ? 'Please enter a valid email address' : undefined"
  >
    <Input placeholder="Enter your email" v-model:value="email" />
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Optical alignment
Use optically-align="start" to apply a negative margin on the child control, aligning its text with the surrounding content. Works with pill inputs. The label, description, and error message stay at the container edge while the control extends past it.
```vue
<div class="w-[400px]">
  <div class="border border-dashed border-alpha/20 rounded-md py-6 px-8">
    <div class="mb-5 text-secondary text-sm">
      {{ opticallyAlign ? 'optically-align="start"' : 'Default' }}
    </div>
    <div class="flex flex-col gap-5">
      <Field label="Full name" :optically-align="opticallyAlign ? 'start' : undefined">
        <Input placeholder="Jane Doe" :pill="pill" />
      </Field>
      <Field
        label="Email"
        description="We'll never share your email."
        error-message="Please enter a valid email address."
        required
        :optically-align="opticallyAlign ? 'start' : undefined"
      >
        <Input placeholder="jane@example.com" :pill="pill" />
      </Field>
    </div>
    <Button class="mt-6" color="primary" variant="soft" :pill="pill">Submit</Button>
  </div>
</div>
```

### Birthday input
A date-of-birth input with inline formatting that inserts MM / DD / YYYY separators as you type, wrapped in a Field for label and accessibility. nextBirthday is a plain helper function of the example.
```vue
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Field label="Birthday">
    <Input
      placeholder="MM / DD / YYYY"
      :value="birthday"
      @update:value="(raw) => (birthday = nextBirthday(raw, birthday))"
      inputmode="numeric"
      maxlength="14"
      autocomplete="off"
    />
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Birthday with validation
Birthday input with error-message on Field: Field automatically passes aria-invalid down and renders FieldError. nextBirthday and isValidBirthday are plain helper functions of the example.
```vue
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Field
    label="Birthday"
    :error-message="birthday && !isValidBirthday(birthday) ? 'Please enter a valid date.' : undefined"
  >
    <Input
      placeholder="MM / DD / YYYY"
      :value="birthday"
      @update:value="(raw) => (birthday = nextBirthday(raw, birthday))"
      inputmode="numeric"
      maxlength="14"
      autocomplete="off"
    />
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Birthday segmented input
Three compact Input controls composed as month/day/year with auto-advance behavior, wrapped in a Field for shared label and accessibility. digitsOnly is a plain helper function of the example.
```vue
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Field label="Birthday">
    <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
      <Input
        :value="month"
        @update:value="(raw) => { month = digitsOnly(raw, 2); if (month.length === 2) $refs.dayInput.focus() }"
        placeholder="MM"
        inputmode="numeric"
        maxlength="2"
        size="md"
        variant="outline"
        autocomplete="off"
        class="w-[56px]"
      />
      <span class="text-secondary">/</span>
      <Input
        ref="dayInput"
        :value="day"
        @update:value="(raw) => { day = digitsOnly(raw, 2); if (day.length === 2) $refs.yearInput.focus() }"
        placeholder="DD"
        inputmode="numeric"
        maxlength="2"
        size="md"
        variant="outline"
        autocomplete="off"
        class="w-[56px]"
      />
      <span class="text-secondary">/</span>
      <Input
        ref="yearInput"
        :value="year"
        @update:value="(raw) => (year = digitsOnly(raw, 4))"
        placeholder="YYYY"
        inputmode="numeric"
        maxlength="4"
        size="md"
        variant="outline"
        autocomplete="off"
        class="w-[80px]"
      />
    </div>
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Birthday with selects
Dropdown birthday picker with Month/Day/Year selects inside a labeled Field group.
```vue
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Field label="Birthday" v-slot="fieldProps">
    <div class="flex gap-2">
      <div class="w-[130px]">
        <Select
          :id="fieldProps.id"
          v-model:value="month"
          :options="birthdayMonthOptions"
          placeholder="Month"
          size="md"
          variant="outline"
        />
      </div>
      <div class="w-[84px]">
        <Select
          v-model:value="day"
          :options="birthdayDayOptions"
          placeholder="Day"
          size="md"
          variant="outline"
          aria-label="Day"
        />
      </div>
      <div class="w-[108px]">
        <Select
          v-model:value="year"
          :options="birthdayYearOptions"
          placeholder="Year"
          size="md"
          variant="outline"
          aria-label="Year"
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  </Field>
</div>
```

## File Upload
Mobile-first file upload with MIME-type and size validation, multi-file selection, error states, and read-only file cards.
Import: import { FileUpload, FileCard, FileCardGroup } from "@plexui/vue/components/FileUpload"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/file-upload
Props:
  - variant ("compact" | "area") default: "compact" — Visual variant: compact button or larger tappable area
  - label (string) — Button label text
  - description (string) — Helper text below the icon (area variant only)
  - accept (string) — Accepted file types (e.g. ".pdf,.png,image/*")
  - multiple (boolean) default: false — Allow selecting multiple files
  - max-files (number) — Maximum number of files. Button disables at limit.
  - max-size (number) — Maximum file size in bytes
  - value / v-model:value (File[]) — Controlled file list; `update:value` fires when files are added or removed
  - @file-reject ((file: File, reason: string) => void) — Fires when a file is rejected (wrong type, too large, over limit)
  - error-message (string) — Error message; sets the invalid visual state
  - invalid (boolean) default: false — Invalid state (auto-set when error-message is provided)
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable all interactions
  FileCard:
    - file (File) — The file to display
    - @remove (() => void) — Remove callback. When omitted, the delete button is hidden.
    - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable the remove button
    - class (string) — Additional CSS class
  FileCardGroup:
    - default slot (VNode[]) — FileCard elements
    - class (string) — Additional CSS class

### Overview
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <FileUpload
    v-model:value="files"
    accept=".pdf,.png,.jpg,.jpeg"
    multiple
    :max-files="5"
    :max-size="10 * 1024 * 1024"
  />
</div>
```

### With files
File list renders below the button in a bordered pill container. Each file shows a type icon, name with middle truncation, size, and a remove button.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <FileUpload
    v-model:value="files"
    accept=".pdf,.png"
    multiple
    :max-files="5"
  />
</div>
```

### Error state
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <FileUpload
    v-model:value="files"
    accept=".pdf"
    multiple
    error-message="Please upload at least 2 documents."
  />
</div>
```

### Disabled
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <FileUpload disabled />
</div>
```

### Read-only
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <FileCardGroup>
    <FileCard :file="file" />
  </FileCardGroup>
</div>
```

### With remove
```vue
<div v-if="file" class="w-[360px]">
  <FileCardGroup>
    <FileCard :file="file" @remove="file = null" />
  </FileCardGroup>
</div>
<div v-else class="w-[360px] text-center text-sm text-tertiary">File removed</div>
```

### Multiple files
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <FileCardGroup>
    <FileCard
      v-for="(file, index) in files"
      :key="file.name + '-' + file.size"
      :file="file"
      @remove="files = files.filter((_, i) => i !== index)"
    />
  </FileCardGroup>
</div>
```

### Read-only list
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <FileCardGroup>
    <FileCard v-for="file in files" :key="file.name + '-' + file.size" :file="file" />
  </FileCardGroup>
</div>
```

## Float Input
Text input whose label floats above the value, with error and helper text, a clear button, a password reveal toggle, and a multi-line variant.
Import: import { FloatingLabelInput } from "@plexui/vue/components/FloatingLabelInput"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/floating-label-input
Props:
  - label (string) — Label text for the floating label
  - value / v-model:value (string) — Controlled value; `update:value` fires on input
  - default-value (string) — Initial value when uncontrolled
  - error-message (string) — Error message to display below the input
  - invalid (boolean) default: false (true if error-message) — Mark the input as invalid
  - description (string | slot) — Helper text below the input
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disables the input
  - read-only (boolean) default: false — Makes the input read-only
  - type (string) default: "text" — Input type; password gets a reveal toggle
  - revealable (boolean) default: true for password without an end adornment — Built-in reveal toggle
  - multiline (boolean) default: false — Render an auto-growing textarea
  - rows (number) default: 2 — Visible lines when multiline
  - allow-autofill-extensions (boolean) default: false — Let password-manager extensions decorate the input
  - start-adornment / end-adornment (slots) — Content before or after the input
  - @clear (() => void) — Shows the clear button and fires when it is clicked
  - @revealed-change ((revealed: boolean) => void) — Fires when the reveal toggle is clicked
  - @autofill (() => void) — Fires when the browser autofills the input

### Overview
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <FloatingLabelInput label="Email address" />
</div>
```

### Filled state
When the input has a value, the label stays floated above the input.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <FloatingLabelInput label="Email address" default-value="jane.doe@example.com" />
</div>
```

### Textarea
Use multiline to render an auto-growing textarea with the same floating-label treatment. The textarea starts at 2 rows and grows automatically as content is added. The border radius adjusts to half the input height.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <FloatingLabelInput
    :key="String(multiline)"
    v-model:value="value"
    label="Message"
    :multiline="multiline"
  />
</div>
```

### With error message
Use the error-message prop to display a validation error below the input. When error-message is provided, the input automatically enters the invalid state.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <FloatingLabelInput
    label="Email address"
    default-value="jane.doe@example"
    invalid
    error-message="Email is not valid."
  />
</div>
```

### With description
Use the description prop (or the description slot) to display helper text below the input.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <FloatingLabelInput
    label="Email address"
    description="We'll never share your email with anyone else."
  />
</div>
```

### With description and error
When both description and error-message are provided, the error appears first (closest to the input), followed by the description.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <FloatingLabelInput
    label="Email address"
    default-value="jane.doe@example"
    invalid
    error-message="Email is not valid."
    description="We'll never share your email with anyone else."
  />
</div>
```

### Clear button
Listen to @clear to display a built-in clear button. The button appears when the input has a value and is not disabled or read-only.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <FloatingLabelInput
    v-model:value="value"
    label="Email address"
    :disabled="disabled"
    :read-only="readOnly"
    @clear="value = ''"
  />
</div>
```

### Disabled
Use the disabled prop to disable the input, greying it out and preventing user interactions.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <FloatingLabelInput label="Email address" default-value="disabled@example.com" :disabled="disabled" />
</div>
```

### Read only
Use the read-only prop to make the input read-only. The user can still select and copy the text.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <FloatingLabelInput label="Email address" default-value="jane.doe@example.com" read-only />
</div>
```

### All states
Overview of all input states.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col items-center gap-8 py-8">
  <div class="flex items-center">
    <div class="text-right text-secondary text-sm mr-8 min-w-[5rem]">Empty</div>
    <FloatingLabelInput label="Email address" class="w-[320px]" />
  </div>
  <div class="flex items-center">
    <div class="text-right text-secondary text-sm mr-8 min-w-[5rem]">Filled</div>
    <FloatingLabelInput label="Email address" default-value="jane.doe@gmail.com" class="w-[320px]" />
  </div>
  <div class="flex items-center">
    <div class="text-right text-secondary text-sm mr-8 min-w-[5rem]">Invalid</div>
    <FloatingLabelInput invalid label="Email address" default-value="invalid@email" class="w-[320px]" />
  </div>
  <div class="flex items-center">
    <div class="text-right text-secondary text-sm mr-8 min-w-[5rem]">Disabled</div>
    <FloatingLabelInput disabled label="Email address" default-value="jane.doe@gmail.com" class="w-[320px]" />
  </div>
  <div class="flex items-center">
    <div class="text-right text-secondary text-sm mr-8 min-w-[5rem]">Read only</div>
    <FloatingLabelInput read-only label="Email address" default-value="readonly@gmail.com" class="w-[320px]" />
  </div>
</div>
```

### Password input
A type="password" field ships a built-in reveal toggle: click the eye to show or hide the value. Set revealable to false to remove it.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <FloatingLabelInput label="Password" type="password" />
</div>
```

### SSN input
A Social Security Number input combining three techniques: an input mask that auto-inserts dashes (XXX-XX-XXXX) with highlighted segment, value masking that hides digits behind bullets (•••-••-••••), and a built-in revealable toggle to reveal the real value. formatSSN, nextSSNDigits and ssnMask are plain helper functions of the example.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <div style="position: relative">
    <FloatingLabelInput
      label="Social Security Number"
      :value="formatSSN(digits)"
      maxlength="11"
      inputmode="numeric"
      autocomplete="off"
      :style="{
        fontFamily: 'var(--font-mono, ui-monospace, monospace)',
        ...(!visible && digits
          ? { color: 'transparent', caretColor: 'transparent' }
          : focused && ssnMask(digits).suffix
            ? { caretColor: 'transparent' }
            : {}),
      }"
      revealable
      @update:value="(raw) => (digits = nextSSNDigits(raw, digits))"
      @focus="focused = true"
      @blur="focused = false"
      @revealed-change="(revealed) => (visible = revealed)"
    />
    <div
      v-if="(!visible && digits) || (focused && ssnMask(digits).suffix)"
      aria-hidden="true"
      style="position: absolute; top: 0; left: 0; right: 0; bottom: 0; display: flex; align-items: center; padding: 0 calc(var(--floating-input-gutter) + 1px); pointer-events: none; font: inherit; font-size: 1rem; line-height: 1.5rem; z-index: 2; font-family: var(--font-mono, ui-monospace, monospace); overflow: hidden"
    >
      <span v-if="!visible && digits" style="white-space: pre">{{ formatSSN(digits).replace(/[0-9]/g, '•') }}</span>
      <span v-else style="visibility: hidden; white-space: pre">{{ formatSSN(digits) }}</span>
      <template v-if="focused && ssnMask(digits).suffix">
        <span v-if="ssnMask(digits).before" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); white-space: pre">{{ ssnMask(digits).before }}</span>
        <span
          v-if="ssnMask(digits).highlight"
          style="background-color: var(--color-background-primary-solid); color: white; white-space: pre; border-radius: 3px; padding: 1px 1px"
        >{{ ssnMask(digits).highlight }}</span>
        <span v-if="ssnMask(digits).after" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); white-space: pre">{{ ssnMask(digits).after }}</span>
      </template>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
```

### Birthday input
A date-of-birth input with inline formatting that inserts separators as you type (MM / DD / YYYY). nextBirthday and birthdayMask are plain helper functions of the example.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <div style="position: relative">
    <FloatingLabelInput
      label="Birthday"
      :value="birthday"
      maxlength="14"
      inputmode="numeric"
      :style="focused && birthdayMask(birthday).suffix ? { caretColor: 'transparent' } : undefined"
      @update:value="(raw) => (birthday = nextBirthday(raw, birthday))"
      @focus="focused = true"
      @blur="focused = false"
    />
    <div
      v-if="focused && birthdayMask(birthday).suffix"
      aria-hidden="true"
      style="position: absolute; top: 0; left: 0; right: 0; bottom: 0; display: flex; align-items: center; padding: 0 calc(var(--floating-input-gutter) + 1px); pointer-events: none; font: inherit; font-size: 1rem; line-height: 1.5rem; z-index: 2; overflow: hidden"
    >
      <span style="visibility: hidden; white-space: pre">{{ birthday }}</span>
      <span v-if="birthdayMask(birthday).before" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); white-space: pre">{{ birthdayMask(birthday).before }}</span>
      <span
        v-if="birthdayMask(birthday).highlight"
        style="background-color: var(--color-background-primary-solid); color: white; white-space: pre; border-radius: 3px; padding: 1px 1px"
      >{{ birthdayMask(birthday).highlight }}</span>
      <span v-if="birthdayMask(birthday).after" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); white-space: pre">{{ birthdayMask(birthday).after }}</span>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
```

### Birthday with validation
Use invalid and an error message to display the birthday validation state. Here the masked input of the Birthday input example is a component, BirthdayMaskedInput, and the error shows in a FieldError below it.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <div class="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
    <component
      :is="BirthdayMaskedInput"
      v-model:value="birthday"
      :invalid="!!birthday && !isValidBirthday(birthday)"
      :aria-describedby="birthday && !isValidBirthday(birthday) ? 'birthday-validation-demo-error' : undefined"
    />
    <FieldError v-if="birthday && !isValidBirthday(birthday)" id="birthday-validation-demo-error">
      Please enter a valid date.
    </FieldError>
  </div>
</div>
```

### Birthday states
Overview of birthday input states.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col items-center gap-8 py-8 w-full">
  <div class="flex items-center w-full max-w-[432px]">
    <div class="text-right text-secondary text-sm mr-8 min-w-[5rem] shrink-0">Empty</div>
    <component :is="BirthdayMaskedInput" v-model:value="emptyBirthday" class="flex-1 min-w-0" />
  </div>
  <div class="flex items-center w-full max-w-[432px]">
    <div class="text-right text-secondary text-sm mr-8 min-w-[5rem] shrink-0">Filled</div>
    <component :is="BirthdayMaskedInput" v-model:value="filledBirthday" class="flex-1 min-w-0" />
  </div>
  <div class="flex items-center w-full max-w-[432px]">
    <div class="text-right text-secondary text-sm mr-8 min-w-[5rem] shrink-0">Invalid</div>
    <component :is="BirthdayMaskedInput" v-model:value="invalidBirthday" invalid class="flex-1 min-w-0" />
  </div>
  <div class="flex items-center w-full max-w-[432px]">
    <div class="text-right text-secondary text-sm mr-8 min-w-[5rem] shrink-0">Disabled</div>
    <component :is="BirthdayMaskedInput" value="01 / 15 / 1995" disabled class="flex-1 min-w-0" />
  </div>
</div>
```

### Select
Use FloatingLabelSelect for plain text dropdowns. The label floats up when a value is selected, matching FloatingLabelInput styling.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <Select
    v-model:value="role"
    :options="roleOptions"
    search-placeholder="Search roles..."
    :list-min-width="360"
    block
    check-position="end"
  >
    <template #trigger="{ open }">
      <FloatingLabelSelect
        label="Role"
        :selected="!!role"
        :open="open"
        :on-clear-click="role ? () => (role = '') : undefined"
      >
        {{ roleOptions.find((option) => option.value === role)?.label }}
      </FloatingLabelSelect>
    </template>
  </Select>
</div>
```

### Multiple selection
Pair FloatingLabelSelect with a multiple Select to collect several values while keeping the floating-label treatment. The trigger shows the selected count and exposes a clear button that resets the whole list.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <Select
    multiple
    clearable
    v-model:value="skills"
    :options="skillOptions"
    search-placeholder="Search skills..."
    :list-min-width="360"
    block
    check-position="end"
  >
    <template #trigger="{ open }">
      <FloatingLabelSelect
        label="Skills"
        :selected="skills.length > 0"
        :open="open"
        :on-clear-click="skills.length > 0 ? () => (skills = []) : undefined"
      >
        {{
          skills.length === 1
            ? skillOptions.find((option) => option.value === skills[0])?.label
            : skills.length > 1
              ? skills.length + ' skills'
              : ''
        }}
      </FloatingLabelSelect>
    </template>
  </Select>
</div>
```

### Country select
Use FloatingLabelSelect alongside FloatingLabelInput for consistent form styling. The select trigger shares the same floating-label visual shell. The label floats up when a value is selected.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <Select
    v-model:value="country"
    :options="countries"
    search-placeholder="Search countries..."
    :list-min-width="360"
    block
    check-position="end"
  >
    <template #optionView="{ value, label }">
      <span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; vertical-align: middle; line-height: 0"><component :is="FlagIcon" :code="value" />{{ label }}</span>
    </template>
    <template #trigger="{ open }">
      <FloatingLabelSelect
        label="Country"
        :selected="!!countryOf(country)"
        :open="open"
        :on-clear-click="countryOf(country) ? () => (country = '') : undefined"
      >
        <span
          v-if="countryOf(country)"
          style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; vertical-align: middle; line-height: 0"
        ><component :is="FlagIcon" :code="country" />{{ countryOf(country).label }}</span>
      </FloatingLabelSelect>
    </template>
  </Select>
</div>
```

### Phone input
A country code selector paired with a FloatingLabelInput that uses the startAdornment slot to show the dial code as a non-editable prefix. The prefix appears when the field is focused or has a value.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px] flex flex-col gap-3">
  <Select
    v-model:value="country"
    :options="phoneCountryOptions"
    search-placeholder="Search countries..."
    :search-predicate="phoneCountrySearchPredicate"
    :list-min-width="360"
    block
    size="3xl"
    check-position="end"
    @change="focusLater($refs.phoneInput)"
  >
    <template #optionView="{ value, label }">
      <span style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; vertical-align: middle; line-height: 0"><component :is="FlagIcon" :code="value" />{{ label }}</span>
    </template>
    <template #trigger="{ open, onToggle }">
      <component :is="PhoneCountryTrigger" :country="country" :open="open" @click="onToggle" />
    </template>
  </Select>
  <FloatingLabelInput
    ref="phoneInput"
    label="Phone number"
    :value="phone"
    @update:value="(raw) => (phone = formatPhoneNumber(raw.replace(/\D/g, ''), country))"
    type="tel"
    inputmode="tel"
  >
    <template #startAdornment>
      <span class="text-tertiary whitespace-nowrap select-none mr-2">{{ dialCodeOf(country) }}</span>
    </template>
  </FloatingLabelInput>
</div>
```

## Indicators
Loading and progress indicators, from circular spinners and determinate progress rings to animated dots, with configurable color and stroke.
Import: import { LoadingIndicator, CircularProgress, LoadingDots } from "@plexui/vue/components/Indicator"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/indicators
  LoadingIndicator:
    - size (number | string) — Size in px (or use --indicator-size CSS variable)
    - stroke-width (number) — Stroke width
  CircularProgress:
    - progress (number) — Static progress 0-100 (disables animation)
    - size (number | string) — Diameter in px
    - stroke-width (number) — Stroke thickness
    - done (boolean) — Completion state for animation
    - max-duration (number) default: 15000 — Duration of the simulated progress in ms
    - track-color (string) — Track color (CSS variable or value)
    - track-active-color (string) — Active segment color

### Overview
The demos restart every few seconds through a done flag that fills the ring before the simulation starts over.
```vue
<div class="flex items-center gap-6">
  <LoadingIndicator :size="20" />
  <CircularProgress :size="24" :done="done" />
  <LoadingDots />
</div>
```

### LoadingIndicator
Simple spinning indicator for general loading states.
```vue
<LoadingIndicator />
```

### Sizing & stroke
By default, the indicator adapts to the surrounding font-size and color of text. You can control the size of the LoadingIndicator using the --indicator-size CSS custom property.
```vue
<LoadingIndicator :size="size" :stroke-width="strokeWidth" />
```

### Color
The indicator defaults to currentcolor, inheriting the surrounding text color. This can also be customized by altering the --indicator-color variable around the DOM scope of the component.
```vue
<div class="flex items-center gap-2 text-danger">
  <LoadingIndicator :size="16" /> <span>Destroying forever...</span>
</div>
```

### Duration
You can control the rotation speed of the spinner with --indicator-rotate-duration.
```vue
<div style="--indicator-rotate-duration: 3s">
  <LoadingIndicator />
</div>
```

### CircularProgress
Circular progress ring with simulated progress animation.
```vue
<CircularProgress :done="done" />
```

### Progress
Use progress to show static progress and prevent the animation.
```vue
<CircularProgress :progress="25" />
```

### Sizing & stroke
Use size and stroke-width. Or use --circular-progress-size and --circular-progress-stroke CSS variables.
```vue
<CircularProgress :size="size" :stroke-width="strokeWidth" :done="done" />
```

### Color
Customize the color of the indicator with the --circular-progress-track-color and --circular-progress-track-active-color variables.
```vue
<CircularProgress
  track-color="var(--alpha-15)"
  track-active-color="var(--gray-1000)"
  :done="done"
/>
```

### LoadingDots
Indicate typing progress with staggered pulsing dot animation.
```vue
<LoadingDots />
```

### Color
The indicator defaults to currentcolor, inheriting the surrounding text color. You can customize the color by providing a different color value or context.
```vue
<div class="text-danger">
  <LoadingDots />
</div>
```

## Input
Text input with start and end adornments, a clear button, a password visibility toggle, and presets for SSN and birthday.
Import: import { Input, InputAdornmentButton } from "@plexui/vue/components/Input"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/input
Props:
  - value / v-model:value (string | number) — Controlled value; `update:value` fires on every input
  - default-value (string | number) — Initial value when uncontrolled
  - variant ("outline" | "soft" | "ghost") default: "outline" — Visual style of the input; ghost has no border and no fill
  - size ("3xs" | "2xs" | "xs" | "sm" | "md" | "lg" | "xl" | "2xl" | "3xl") default: "md" — Controls the size of the input
  - gutter-size (string) — Controls gutter on the edges; defaults to value from size
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disables the input visually and from interactions
  - read-only (boolean) default: false — Makes the input read-only and hides the clear button
  - invalid (boolean) default: false — Mark the input as invalid
  - allow-autofill-extensions (boolean) default: false (true with name or type="password") — Allow autofill extensions to appear in the input
  - auto-select (boolean) default: false — Select all contents of the input when mounted
  - startAdornment / endAdornment (slots) — Content rendered at the start or end of the input
  - @clear (() => void) — When set, displays a clear button that appears when the input has a value
  - @autofill (() => void) — Fires when the browser autofills the input
  - pill (boolean) default: false — Fully rounded pill shape
  - optically-align ("start" | "end") — Applies a negative margin using the current gutter to optically align with surrounding content
  InputAdornmentButton:
    - color (SemanticColor) — Tint of the pressed (aria-pressed="true") state; the neutral primary-soft tint when unset
    - type (string) default: "button" — Button type

### Overview
```vue
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Input placeholder="Enter text..." />
</div>
```

### Variants
Use variant to stylize the input. ghost has no border and no fill, for a field its container already frames, such as the search line at the top of a dialog; it still turns red when invalid.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2 w-[320px]">
  <Input placeholder="Enter text..." variant="outline" />
  <Input placeholder="Enter text..." variant="soft" />
```
