```vue
  <Input placeholder="Enter text..." variant="ghost" />
</div>
```

### Sizing & roundness
Sizing of the input is controlled with the size and gutter-size props. size sets the input to a specific control height, and gutter-size sets the horizontal padding. Each size provides default values for gutter-size, as well as font-size. Each of these values can be overridden independently.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2.5 justify-start items-start w-[320px]">
  <Input
    placeholder="Enter text..."
    :size="size"
    :gutter-size="gutterSize === 'auto' ? undefined : gutterSize"
    :pill="pill"
  />
  <Input
    placeholder="Search..."
    :size="size"
    :gutter-size="gutterSize === 'auto' ? undefined : gutterSize"
    :pill="pill"
  >
    <template #startAdornment>
      <Search class="fill-tertiary" />
    </template>
  </Input>
  <Input
    v-model:value="username"
    placeholder="Username"
    maxlength="16"
    :size="size"
    :gutter-size="gutterSize === 'auto' ? undefined : gutterSize"
    :pill="pill"
  >
    <template #endAdornment>
      <span class="mt-[1.5px] tabular-nums text-tertiary text-[.875em]">{{ username.length }}/16</span>
    </template>
  </Input>
</div>
```

### Start adornment
You can add arbitrary content to the beginning of an input with the startAdornment slot, like a search icon. Ideally, the spacing will look pretty good by default. If you need to customize the spacing: gutter-size determines the amount of spacing on the edges of the container, which you can fine-tune as needed (if you need precise spacing not supported by gutter-size, use negative left margin on your adornment directly, e.g. -ml-2). The spacing between the adornment and the input is controlled with gap on the container, which you can customize as needed with Tailwind classes.
```vue
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Input placeholder="Start adornment">
    <template #startAdornment>
      <Search class="fill-tertiary" />
    </template>
  </Input>
</div>
```

### Clear button
Listen to @clear to display a built-in clear button. The button appears when the input has a value and is not disabled or read-only.
```vue
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Input
    v-model:value="value"
    placeholder="Enter text..."
    :pill="pill"
    :size="size"
    @clear="value = ''"
  />
</div>
```

### End adornment
You can add arbitrary content to the end of an input with the endAdornment slot, like a character counter or custom icon. The same spacing guidance from startAdornment applies here.
```vue
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Input v-model:value="username" placeholder="Username" maxlength="16">
    <template #endAdornment>
      <span class="mt-[1.5px] tabular-nums text-tertiary text-[.875em]">{{ username.length }}/16</span>
    </template>
  </Input>
</div>
```

### Password with visibility toggle
Put an InputAdornmentButton in the endAdornment slot to switch between type="password" and type="text". Works across all sizes, variants, and with pill.
```vue
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Input
    placeholder="Password"
    :type="visible ? 'text' : 'password'"
    :size="size"
    :variant="variant"
    :pill="pill"
  >
    <template #endAdornment>
      <InputAdornmentButton
        :aria-label="visible ? 'Hide' : 'Show'"
        :aria-pressed="visible"
        @click="visible = !visible"
      >
        <EyeOff v-if="visible" />
        <Eye v-else />
      </InputAdornmentButton>
    </template>
  </Input>
</div>
```

### SSN input
A masked SSN field that formats input as XXX-XX-XXXX, hides digits behind bullets when masked, and reveals the value via an InputAdornmentButton in the endAdornment slot. An inline mask suffix highlights the next expected segment. Adapts to all sizes automatically. formatSSN, nextSSNDigits, ssnMask and measureInput are plain helper functions of the example.
```vue
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <div style="position: relative">
    <Input
      :value="formatSSN(digits)"
      :placeholder="focused && ssnMask(digits).suffix ? undefined : 'Social Security Number'"
      :size="size"
      :variant="variant"
      :pill="pill"
      maxlength="11"
      inputmode="numeric"
      :style="{
        fontFamily: 'var(--font-mono, ui-monospace, monospace)',
        ...(!visible && digits
          ? { color: 'transparent', caretColor: 'transparent' }
          : focused && ssnMask(digits).suffix
            ? { caretColor: 'transparent' }
            : {}),
      }"
      @update:value="(raw) => (digits = nextSSNDigits(raw, digits))"
      @focus="focused = true"
      @blur="focused = false"
      @vue:mounted="(vnode) => Object.assign(metrics, measureInput(vnode))"
      @vue:updated="(vnode) => Object.assign(metrics, measureInput(vnode))"
    >
      <template #endAdornment>
        <InputAdornmentButton
          :aria-label="visible ? 'Hide' : 'Show'"
          :aria-pressed="visible"
          @click="visible = !visible"
        >
          <EyeOff v-if="visible" />
          <Eye v-else />
        </InputAdornmentButton>
      </template>
    </Input>
    <div
      v-if="(!visible && digits) || (focused && ssnMask(digits).suffix)"
      aria-hidden="true"
      :style="{
        position: 'absolute',
        top: '0',
        left: '0',
        right: '0',
        bottom: '0',
        display: 'flex',
        alignItems: 'center',
        padding: '0 ' + metrics.padding,
        pointerEvents: 'none',
        fontSize: metrics.fontSize,
        lineHeight: metrics.lineHeight,
        zIndex: 2,
        fontFamily: 'var(--font-mono, ui-monospace, monospace)',
        overflow: 'hidden',
      }"
    >
      <span v-if="!visible && digits" style="white-space: pre">{{ formatSSN(digits).replace(/[0-9]/g, '•') }}</span>
      <span v-else style="visibility: hidden; white-space: pre">{{ formatSSN(digits) }}</span>
      <template v-if="focused && ssnMask(digits).suffix">
        <span v-if="ssnMask(digits).before" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); white-space: pre">{{ ssnMask(digits).before }}</span>
        <span
          v-if="ssnMask(digits).highlight"
          style="background-color: var(--color-background-primary-solid); color: white; white-space: pre; border-radius: 3px; padding: 1px 1px"
        >{{ ssnMask(digits).highlight }}</span>
        <span v-if="ssnMask(digits).after" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); white-space: pre">{{ ssnMask(digits).after }}</span>
      </template>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
```

### Birthday input
A date-of-birth input with inline formatting that inserts separators as you type (MM / DD / YYYY). An inline mask suffix highlights the next expected segment. Works across all sizes, variants, and with pill. nextBirthday, birthdayMask and measureInput are plain helper functions of the example.
```vue
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <div style="position: relative">
    <Input
      :value="birthday"
      :placeholder="focused && birthdayMask(birthday).suffix ? undefined : 'Birthday (MM / DD / YYYY)'"
      :size="size"
      :variant="variant"
      :pill="pill"
      maxlength="14"
      inputmode="numeric"
      :style="focused && birthdayMask(birthday).suffix ? { caretColor: 'transparent' } : undefined"
      @update:value="(raw) => (birthday = nextBirthday(raw, birthday))"
      @focus="focused = true"
      @blur="focused = false"
      @vue:mounted="(vnode) => Object.assign(metrics, measureInput(vnode))"
      @vue:updated="(vnode) => Object.assign(metrics, measureInput(vnode))"
    />
    <div
      v-if="focused && birthdayMask(birthday).suffix"
      aria-hidden="true"
      :style="{
        position: 'absolute',
        top: '0',
        left: '0',
        right: '0',
        bottom: '0',
        display: 'flex',
        alignItems: 'center',
        padding: '0 ' + metrics.padding,
        pointerEvents: 'none',
        fontSize: metrics.fontSize,
        lineHeight: metrics.lineHeight,
        zIndex: 2,
        overflow: 'hidden',
      }"
    >
      <span style="visibility: hidden; white-space: pre">{{ birthday }}</span>
      <span v-if="birthdayMask(birthday).before" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); white-space: pre">{{ birthdayMask(birthday).before }}</span>
      <span
        v-if="birthdayMask(birthday).highlight"
        style="background-color: var(--color-background-primary-solid); color: white; white-space: pre; border-radius: 3px; padding: 1px 1px"
      >{{ birthdayMask(birthday).highlight }}</span>
      <span v-if="birthdayMask(birthday).after" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); white-space: pre">{{ birthdayMask(birthday).after }}</span>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
```

### Birthday with validation
Use invalid and FieldError to display a validation state when the entered date is not valid.
```vue
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <div class="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
    <div style="position: relative">
      <Input
        :value="birthday"
        :placeholder="focused && birthdayMask(birthday).suffix ? undefined : 'Birthday (MM / DD / YYYY)'"
        :size="size"
        :variant="variant"
        :pill="pill"
        maxlength="14"
        inputmode="numeric"
        :invalid="!!birthday && !isValidBirthday(birthday)"
        :style="focused && birthdayMask(birthday).suffix ? { caretColor: 'transparent' } : undefined"
        @update:value="(raw) => (birthday = nextBirthday(raw, birthday))"
        @focus="focused = true"
        @blur="focused = false"
        @vue:mounted="(vnode) => Object.assign(metrics, measureInput(vnode))"
        @vue:updated="(vnode) => Object.assign(metrics, measureInput(vnode))"
      />
      <div
        v-if="focused && birthdayMask(birthday).suffix"
        aria-hidden="true"
        :style="{
          position: 'absolute',
          top: '0',
          left: '0',
          right: '0',
          bottom: '0',
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          padding: '0 ' + metrics.padding,
          pointerEvents: 'none',
          fontSize: metrics.fontSize,
          lineHeight: metrics.lineHeight,
          zIndex: 2,
          overflow: 'hidden',
        }"
      >
        <span style="visibility: hidden; white-space: pre">{{ birthday }}</span>
        <span v-if="birthdayMask(birthday).before" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); white-space: pre">{{ birthdayMask(birthday).before }}</span>
        <span
          v-if="birthdayMask(birthday).highlight"
          style="background-color: var(--color-background-primary-solid); color: white; white-space: pre; border-radius: 3px; padding: 1px 1px"
        >{{ birthdayMask(birthday).highlight }}</span>
        <span v-if="birthdayMask(birthday).after" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); white-space: pre">{{ birthdayMask(birthday).after }}</span>
      </div>
    </div>
    <FieldError v-if="birthday && !isValidBirthday(birthday)" id="input-birthday-validation-demo-error">
      Please enter a valid date.
    </FieldError>
  </div>
</div>
```

### Disabled
Use the disabled prop to disable the input, greying it out and preventing user interactions.
```vue
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Input placeholder="Jane Doe" :disabled="disabled" default-value="Jane Doe" />
</div>
```

### Invalid
Use the invalid prop to mark the input as invalid. You decide how this state is removed, whether that's through @focus, @update:value, a submit handler, etc. Default visual styles will be applied, based on the variant. Additionally, [data-invalid] is applied to the container for custom styling.
```vue
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Input placeholder="Invalid input" :invalid="invalid" />
</div>
```

### Auto select text
Pass auto-select to select all text in the input, useful when pairing with other copy actions. This prop behaves like an imperative action when toggled and allows selection to change.
```vue
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Input placeholder="Add text..." :auto-select="autoSelect" default-value="Toggle to auto select" />
</div>
```

### Autofill extensions
By default, autofill extensions are prevented by Input. For the occasions where you want browser extensions to be present, pass allow-autofill-extensions to remove the disabling. When name is passed, or type is set to "password", this value will default to true.
```vue
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Input
    :key="String(allowAutofillExtensions)"
    name="email"
    :allow-autofill-extensions="allowAutofillExtensions"
    :placeholder="allowAutofillExtensions ? 'Allowed' : 'Not allowed'"
  />
</div>
```

### With button
You can pair Input and Button components together by using the same size value.
```vue
<div class="flex w-[320px] gap-2">
  <Input placeholder="jane.doe@gmail.com" :size="size" :variant="variant" />
  <Button color="primary" :size="size" :variant="variant">Subscribe</Button>
</div>
```

### Optical alignment
```vue
<div class="w-[400px]">
  <div class="border border-dashed border-alpha/20 rounded-md py-6 px-8">
    <div class="mb-5 text-secondary text-sm">
      {{ opticallyAlign ? 'optically-align="start"' : 'Default' }}
    </div>
    <div class="flex flex-col gap-5">
      <Field label="Full name" :optically-align="opticallyAlign ? 'start' : undefined">
        <Input placeholder="Jane Doe" :pill="pill" />
      </Field>
      <Field
        label="Email"
        description="We'll never share your email."
        error-message="Please enter a valid email address."
        required
        :optically-align="opticallyAlign ? 'start' : undefined"
      >
        <Input placeholder="jane@example.com" :pill="pill" />
      </Field>
    </div>
    <Button class="mt-6" color="primary" variant="soft" :pill="pill">Submit</Button>
  </div>
</div>
```

## Input Group
Input or textarea with icons, text, buttons and menus attached inside one frame, inline or as header and footer rows, in every input size and style.
Import: import { InputGroup, InputGroupAddon, InputGroupButton, InputGroupInput, InputGroupText, InputGroupTextarea } from "@plexui/vue/components/InputGroup"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/input-group
Props:
  - size ("3xs" | "2xs" | "xs" | "sm" | "md" | "lg" | "xl" | "2xl" | "3xl") default: "md" — Control height, on the same scale as Input (22px to 48px)
  - variant ("outline" | "soft") default: "outline" — Visual style of the frame
  - pill (boolean) default: false — Fully rounded frame, with pill buttons inside
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disables the control and the buttons inside
  - invalid (boolean) default: false — Invalid border; sets aria-invalid on the control
  - class (string) — Applied to the root <div>
  InputGroupAddon:
    - align ("inline-start" | "inline-end" | "block-start" | "block-end") default: "inline-start" — Before or after the control on its line, or a row above or below it
    - class (string) — Applied to the addon <div>

### Overview
Put an InputGroupInput and one or more InputGroupAddons inside an InputGroup. The group draws the frame and passes size, pill, disabled and invalid down to the control and the buttons.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2.5 w-[320px]">
  <InputGroup :size="size" :variant="variant" :pill="pill" :disabled="disabled" :invalid="invalid">
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="Search..." />
    <InputGroupAddon>
      <Search />
    </InputGroupAddon>
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
      <InputGroupText>12 results</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup :size="size" :variant="variant" :pill="pill" :disabled="disabled" :invalid="invalid">
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="example" />
    <InputGroupAddon>
      <InputGroupText>https://</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
      <InputGroupText>.com</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup :size="size" :variant="variant" :pill="pill" :disabled="disabled" :invalid="invalid">
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="Ask anything..." />
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
      <InputGroupButton variant="solid" color="primary" uniform aria-label="Send">
        <ArrowUpSm />
      </InputGroupButton>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
</div>
```

### Variants
Use variant to stylize the group, like Input.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2.5 w-[320px]">
  <InputGroup variant="outline">
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="Outline" />
    <InputGroupAddon>
      <Search />
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup variant="soft">
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="Soft" />
    <InputGroupAddon>
      <Search />
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
</div>
```

### Sizes
Every Input size from 3xs to 3xl. Icons, text and InputGroupButton scale with the group: buttons in inline addons fit the control height and keep a 4px inset.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2.5 w-[360px]">
  <div v-for="size in sizes" :key="size" class="flex items-center gap-4">
    <span class="w-8 shrink-0 text-right text-sm text-tertiary">{{ size }}</span>
    <InputGroup :size="size" :pill="pill">
      <InputGroupInput placeholder="Search..." />
      <InputGroupAddon>
        <Search />
      </InputGroupAddon>
      <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
        <InputGroupButton>Search</InputGroupButton>
      </InputGroupAddon>
    </InputGroup>
  </div>
</div>
```

### Align
Use align on InputGroupAddon to place it before (inline-start, the default) or after (inline-end) the control, or as a row above (block-start) or below (block-end) it. Place addons after the control in the markup so the tab order starts with the field; align handles the visual position.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2.5 w-[320px]">
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="inline-start" />
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-start">
      <Search />
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="inline-end" />
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
      <InfoCircle />
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="Ada Lovelace" />
    <InputGroupAddon align="block-start">
      <InputGroupText>Full name</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="0.00" />
    <InputGroupAddon align="block-end">
      <InputGroupText>Amount in USD</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
</div>
```

### Text
Use InputGroupText for prefixes, suffixes, units and counters. Clicking text or icons in an addon focuses the field.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2.5 w-[320px]">
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="0.00" inputmode="decimal" />
    <InputGroupAddon>
      <InputGroupText>$</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
      <InputGroupText>USD</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="example" />
    <InputGroupAddon>
      <InputGroupText>https://</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
      <InputGroupText>.com</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="username" />
    <InputGroupAddon>
      <Email />
    </InputGroupAddon>
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
      <InputGroupText>@company.com</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupTextarea v-model:value="post" placeholder="What's happening?" maxlength="280" />
    <InputGroupAddon align="block-end">
      <InputGroupText>{{ 280 - post.length }} characters left</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
</div>
```

### Button
InputGroupButton is a Button that defaults to ghost and secondary and takes its size from the group. Pass uniform for icon buttons.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2.5 w-[320px]">
  <InputGroup :size="size" :pill="pill">
    <InputGroupInput default-value="plexui.com/r/input-group" readonly />
    <InputGroupAddon>
      <InputGroupText>https://</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
      <InputGroupButton uniform aria-label="Copy link">
        <Copy />
      </InputGroupButton>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup :size="size" :pill="pill">
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="Search the docs..." />
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
      <InputGroupButton variant="soft">Search</InputGroupButton>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup :size="size" :pill="pill">
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="Card number" inputmode="numeric" />
    <InputGroupAddon>
      <CreditCard />
    </InputGroupAddon>
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
      <InputGroupButton variant="solid" color="primary">Pay</InputGroupButton>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
</div>
```

### Dropdown
Wrap an InputGroupButton in a Menu to pick a unit of measurement next to the value.
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref } from "vue"
import { ChevronDownMd } from "@plexui/vue/components/Icon"
import {
  InputGroup,
  InputGroupAddon,
  InputGroupButton,
  InputGroupInput,
  InputGroupText,
} from "@plexui/vue/components/InputGroup"
import { Menu, MenuCheckboxItem, MenuContent, MenuTrigger } from "@plexui/vue/components/Menu"

const UNITS = [
  { value: "kg", label: "Kilograms" },
  { value: "lb", label: "Pounds" },
  { value: "st", label: "Stones" },
]
const unit = ref("kg")
</script>

<template>
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupInput default-value="72" inputmode="decimal" aria-label="Weight" />
    <InputGroupAddon>
      <InputGroupText>Weight</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
      <Menu>
        <MenuTrigger>
          <InputGroupButton aria-label="Weight unit">
            {{ unit }}<ChevronDownMd />
          </InputGroupButton>
        </MenuTrigger>
        <MenuContent align="end" :min-width="160">
          <MenuCheckboxItem
            v-for="item in UNITS"
            :key="item.value"
            :checked="unit === item.value"
            indicator-variant="ghost"
            @update:checked="unit = item.value"
          >
            {{ item.label }} ({{ item.value }})
          </MenuCheckboxItem>
        </MenuContent>
      </Menu>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
</template>
```

### Spinner
Show a LoadingIndicator in an addon while a value is searched or saved.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2.5 w-[320px]">
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupInput default-value="Searching..." readonly />
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
      <LoadingIndicator />
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupInput default-value="Processing..." readonly />
    <InputGroupAddon>
      <LoadingIndicator />
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupInput default-value="Saving changes" readonly />
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
      <InputGroupText>Saving...</InputGroupText>
      <LoadingIndicator />
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
</div>
```

### Textarea
With InputGroupTextarea, use block-start and block-end addons for a header and a footer. Utility classes such as ml-auto push a button to the far side.
```vue
<div class="w-[400px]">
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupTextarea placeholder="console.log('Hello, world!');" :rows="4" class="font-mono" />
    <InputGroupAddon align="block-start">
      <InputGroupText>
        <FileCode />script.js
      </InputGroupText>
      <InputGroupButton uniform aria-label="Copy" class="ml-auto">
        <Copy />
      </InputGroupButton>
    </InputGroupAddon>
    <InputGroupAddon align="block-end">
      <InputGroupText>Line 1, Column 1</InputGroupText>
      <InputGroupButton variant="solid" color="primary" class="ml-auto">
        Run<Play />
      </InputGroupButton>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
</div>
```

## Kbd
A key or a keyboard shortcut, such as ⌘K, set inline in text, in a tooltip or at the end of a menu item.
Import: import { Kbd, KbdGroup } from "@plexui/vue/components/Kbd"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/kbd
Props:
  - size ("xs" | "sm" | "md") default: "sm" — Height and text size: 16px, 20px or 24px
  - variant ("soft" | "outline") default: "soft" — soft fills the key, outline draws its edge; both mix the text color
  - class (string) — Applied to the kbd element
  - default slot (VNode[]) — The key

### Overview
Kbd renders a {''} element: the semantic tag for keyboard input. It sits on the text baseline, so it can stand in a sentence.
```vue
<p class="text-sm text-secondary">
  Press <Kbd>/</Kbd> to search, <Kbd>Esc</Kbd> to close.
</p>
```

### Shortcut
Use KbdGroup for keys pressed together. Each key keeps its own frame.
```vue
<KbdGroup>
  <Kbd>⌘</Kbd>
  <Kbd>K</Kbd>
</KbdGroup>
<KbdGroup>
  <Kbd>Ctrl</Kbd>
  <Kbd>Shift</Kbd>
  <Kbd>P</Kbd>
</KbdGroup>
```

### Sizes and variants
Three sizes: xs (16px) for tooltips and menu items, sm (20px, default) for text, md (24px) next to larger controls. soft (default) fills the key, outline draws its edge. Both mix the surrounding text color, so a key reads on a page, in a dark tooltip and in a highlighted menu item alike.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col items-center gap-4">
  <div v-for="variant in ['soft', 'outline']" :key="variant" class="flex items-center gap-4">
    <Kbd size="xs" :variant="variant">⌘K</Kbd>
    <Kbd size="sm" :variant="variant">⌘K</Kbd>
    <Kbd size="md" :variant="variant">⌘K</Kbd>
  </div>
</div>
```

### In a tooltip and a menu
Show a control's shortcut in its tooltip, or at the end of a menu item. Kbd only shows the keys: announce the shortcut to assistive technology with aria-keyshortcuts on the control that has it.
```vue
<Tooltip>
  <template #content>
    <span class="flex items-center gap-2">
      Search
      <KbdGroup>
        <Kbd size="xs">⌘</Kbd>
        <Kbd size="xs">K</Kbd>
      </KbdGroup>
    </span>
  </template>
  <Button color="secondary" variant="outline" uniform aria-label="Search" aria-keyshortcuts="Meta+K">
    <Search />
  </Button>
</Tooltip>
<Menu>
  <MenuTrigger>
    <Button color="secondary" variant="soft">Edit</Button>
  </MenuTrigger>
  <MenuContent :min-width="220">
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}">
      <span class="flex-1">Undo</span>
      <Kbd size="xs" variant="outline">⌘Z</Kbd>
    </MenuItem>
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}">
      <span class="flex-1">Redo</span>
      <Kbd size="xs" variant="outline">⇧⌘Z</Kbd>
    </MenuItem>
  </MenuContent>
</Menu>
```

## Label
Accessible form label that points at any control by its id, so clicking it focuses or toggles the control, with consistent typography across forms.
Import: import { Label } from "@plexui/vue/components/Label"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/label
Props:
  - for (string) — id of the associated control. Clicks on the label focus or activate the control.
  - class (string) — Applied to the root <label> element
  - default slot (VNode[]) — Label content

### Overview
Clicking the label toggles the checkbox it points at. Here accepted starts as true.
```vue
<div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px">
  <Checkbox id="label-demo-terms" v-model:checked="accepted" />
  <Label for="label-demo-terms">Accept terms and conditions</Label>
</div>
```

### Label in Field
For stacked form rows with descriptions, errors, and horizontal layouts, use the Field component. It renders the label internally, hooks up for automatically, and handles the surrounding spacing.
```vue
<div style="width: 100%; max-width: 420px">
  <div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 16px">
    <CardHeader>
      <CardTitle>Payment Method</CardTitle>
      <CardDescription>All transactions are secure and encrypted</CardDescription>
    </CardHeader>

    <Field label="Name on Card">
      <Input placeholder="Aisha Cooper" />
    </Field>

    <Field label="Card Number" description="Enter your 16-digit card number">
      <Input placeholder="1234 5678 9012 3456" inputmode="numeric" />
    </Field>

    <div style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr 1fr; gap: 16px">
      <Field label="Month">
        <Select v-model:value="month" :options="monthOptions" placeholder="MM" />
      </Field>
      <Field label="Year">
        <Select v-model:value="year" :options="yearOptions" placeholder="YYYY" />
      </Field>
      <Field label="CVV">
        <Input placeholder="123" inputmode="numeric" />
      </Field>
    </div>

    <Separator />

    <CardHeader>
      <CardTitle>Billing Address</CardTitle>
      <CardDescription>The billing address associated with your payment method</CardDescription>
    </CardHeader>

    <Checkbox v-model:checked="sameAsShipping" label="Same as shipping address" />

    <Separator />

    <Field label="Comments">
      <Textarea placeholder="Add any additional comments" :rows="3" />
    </Field>

    <div style="display: flex; gap: 8px">
      <Button color="primary" variant="solid">Submit</Button>
      <Button color="secondary" variant="outline">Cancel</Button>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
```

## Markdown
Markdown renderer for rich formatted content, with headings, lists, tables, code blocks, and KaTeX math expressions out of the box.
Import: import { Markdown } from "@plexui/vue/components/Markdown"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/markdown
Props:
  - default slot (string) — Markdown source (text only; interpolate a string to keep its newlines)
  - source (string) — Markdown source, used when the default slot is empty
  - include-math (boolean) default: false — Enable LaTeX math rendering

### Overview
Use Markdown to render rich markdown text. The markdown is the text of the default slot: interpolate a string ({"{{ markdown }}"}) so its newlines are kept, or pass it with the source prop.
```vue
<Markdown>{{ markdownContent }}</Markdown>
```

### Math
Use include-math to enable LaTeX rendering.
```vue
<Markdown include-math>{{ markdownWithLatex }}</Markdown>
```

## Markdown Editor
Compact rich text editor powered by Tiptap, embedded in a form field, with a markdown formatting toolbar, variants, and an invalid state.
Import: import { MarkdownEditor } from "@plexui/vue/components/MarkdownEditor"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/markdown-editor
Props:
  - variant ("outline" | "soft") default: "outline" — Visual style of the editor
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable the editor
  - invalid (boolean) default: false — Mark as invalid
  - placeholder (string) — Placeholder text when empty
  - value / v-model:value (string) — Controlled markdown value; `update:value` fires on every change
  - default-value (string) — Initial markdown for uncontrolled mode
  - @change ((markdown: string) => void) — Called on content change
  - #toolbarEnd (slot) — Content rendered at the end of the toolbar
  - rows (number) default: 1 — Minimum visible rows

### Placeholder
Use placeholder to display hint text when the editor is empty.
```vue
<div class="w-[500px]">
  <MarkdownEditor placeholder="Enter text..." />
</div>
```

### All formatting
Example with all supported formatting: bold, italic, links, ordered lists, and unordered lists. Click inside formatted text to see toolbar buttons toggle.
```vue
<div class="w-[500px]">
  <MarkdownEditor :default-value="allFormattingMd" />
</div>
```

### With Field
Wrap with Field to add label, description, and error handling. The editor receives id and aria-describedby automatically.
```vue
<div class="w-[500px]">
  <Field label="Description" description="Supports bold, italic, lists, and links.">
    <MarkdownEditor placeholder="Enter description..." />
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Toolbar end slot
Use the #toolbarEnd slot to render custom content at the end of the toolbar, for example an &ldquo;Edit translations&rdquo; button. markdownEditorStyles.toolbarButton gives it the style of the built-in toolbar buttons.
```vue
<div class="w-[500px]">
  <Field label="Text markdown">
    <MarkdownEditor placeholder="Enter text...">
      <template #toolbarEnd>
        <button
          type="button"
          :class="markdownEditorStyles.toolbarButton"
          aria-label="Edit translations"
          title="Edit translations"
        >
          <Translate />
        </button>
      </template>
    </MarkdownEditor>
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Variants
Use variant to switch between outline (default) and soft styles.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-4 w-[500px]">
  <Field label="Outline">
    <MarkdownEditor placeholder="Outline variant..." variant="outline" />
  </Field>
  <Field label="Soft">
    <MarkdownEditor placeholder="Soft variant..." variant="soft" />
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Disabled
Use disabled to prevent editing and grey out the component.
```vue
<div class="w-[500px]">
  <MarkdownEditor disabled default-value="This content is **read-only**." />
</div>
```

### Invalid
Use invalid or wrap with Field and pass error-message to show an error state.
```vue
<div class="w-[500px]">
  <Field label="Bio" error-message="This field is required.">
    <MarkdownEditor placeholder="Tell us about yourself..." invalid />
  </Field>
</div>
```

## Menu
Dropdown menu for structured actions with grouped items, submenus, checkbox and radio items, and horizontal layouts.
Import: import { Menu, MenuContent, MenuItem, MenuLink, MenuSeparator, MenuTrigger } from "@plexui/vue/components/Menu"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/menu
Props:
  - force-open (boolean) — Keep menu open (controlled)
  - modal (boolean) default: false — Whether menu is modal (focus trap)
  - @open (() => void) — Emitted when menu opens
  - @close (() => void) — Emitted when menu closes
  MenuTrigger:
    - disabled (boolean) default: false — Prevent the menu from opening
  MenuContent:
    - width (number | "auto") — Panel width (numbers are pixels)
    - min-width (number | "auto") default: 300 — Minimum panel width
    - max-height (number) — Maximum panel height, in pixels
    - side ("top" | "right" | "bottom" | "left") default: "bottom" — Preferred placement
    - side-offset (number) default: 5 — Distance from the trigger, in pixels
    - align ("start" | "center" | "end") — Alignment relative to trigger
    - align-offset (number) — Offset from the start or end alignment, in pixels
    - layout ("vertical" | "horizontal") default: "vertical" — Layout direction. Use horizontal for icon toolbars
    - size ("sm" | "md" | "lg") default: "md" — Size of horizontal menu items (icon size + padding)
    - @close-auto-focus ((event: Event) => void) — Emitted before focus returns to the trigger; call event.preventDefault() to keep it from moving there
  MenuItem:
    - description (string) — Second line under the label (or the description slot). The label turns semibold on its own
    - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable the item
    - danger (boolean) default: false — Destructive action: the item is drawn in the danger color
    - @select ((event: Event) => void) — Called when the item is selected; call event.preventDefault() to keep the menu open
    - @click ((event: MouseEvent) => void) — Emitted when the item is clicked
  MenuCheckboxItem:
    - checked / v-model:checked (boolean | "indeterminate") default: false — Checked state
    - @checked-change ((checked: boolean) => void) — Emitted with update:checked when the checkbox toggles
    - @select ((event: Event) => void) — Call event.preventDefault() to keep the menu open
    - indicator-position ("start" | "end") default: "end" — Checkbox position
    - indicator-variant ("solid" | "ghost") default: "solid" — Indicator style
    - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable the item

### Overview
```vue
<Menu>
  <MenuTrigger>
    <Button color="primary" size="lg" variant="ghost">Sample menu <ChevronDownMd /></Button>
  </MenuTrigger>
  <MenuContent :width="210" :min-width="210">
    <MenuItem description="sam.smith@gmail.com">Sam Smith</MenuItem>
    <MenuSeparator />
    <MenuLink href="/settings">Your profile</MenuLink>
    <MenuLink href="#">Terms & policies</MenuLink>
    <MenuItem disabled @select="() => {}">Feature flags</MenuItem>
    <MenuSeparator />
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}">Logout</MenuItem>
  </MenuContent>
</Menu>
```

### Natural sizing
Menu supports several sizing strategies:Natural sizing: use min-width="auto" to have the menu fit naturally to the size of the content.Fixed size: use width and min-width to set the menu to a fixed size, regardless of content size.Flexible sizing: min-width can be used by itself to describe a menu that must be a given size, but can grow larger depending on its content.Max sizing: apply max-width to the children of MenuContent. maxWidth isn't exposed as a prop because it's reserved for the custom properties used in collision detection.
```vue
<Menu>
  <MenuTrigger>
    <Button color="primary" size="lg" variant="ghost" class="font-semibold gap-1.5"><Tools /> Tools</Button>
  </MenuTrigger>
  <MenuContent min-width="auto">
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><Functions :height="18" :width="18" /> Function</MenuItem>
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><Search :height="18" :width="18" /> File Search</MenuItem>
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><Globe :height="18" :width="18" /> Web Search</MenuItem>
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><Code :height="18" :width="18" /> Code Interpreter</MenuItem>
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><ImageSquare :height="18" :width="18" /> Image Generation</MenuItem>
  </MenuContent>
</Menu>
```

### Horizontal (icons only)
Set layout="horizontal" on MenuContent for an iOS-style icon toolbar.
```vue
<Menu>
  <MenuTrigger>
    <Button color="primary" size="lg" variant="ghost">Actions <ChevronDownMd /></Button>
  </MenuTrigger>
  <MenuContent layout="horizontal" :size="size" min-width="auto">
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><EditPencil /></MenuItem>
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><SettingsCog /></MenuItem>
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><Play /></MenuItem>
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><UnfoldHorizontal /></MenuItem>
  </MenuContent>
</Menu>
```

### Horizontal (icons with labels)
Add text labels below icons for a labeled toolbar. The horizontal layout automatically stacks icon and text vertically within each item.
```vue
<Menu>
  <MenuTrigger>
    <Button color="primary" size="lg" variant="ghost">Actions <ChevronDownMd /></Button>
  </MenuTrigger>
  <MenuContent layout="horizontal" :size="size" min-width="auto">
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><EditPencil /> Edit</MenuItem>
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><SettingsCog /> Settings</MenuItem>
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><Play /> Play</MenuItem>
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><UnfoldHorizontal /> Expand</MenuItem>
  </MenuContent>
</Menu>
```

### Grouped items
Use MenuGroup and MenuLabel to organize related items into labeled sections.
```vue
<Menu>
  <MenuTrigger>
    <Button color="primary" size="lg" variant="ghost" class="font-semibold gap-1.5"><FileDocument /> Templates <ChevronDownMd /></Button>
  </MenuTrigger>
  <MenuContent :min-width="220">
    <MenuGroup>
      <MenuLabel>Active</MenuLabel>
      <MenuLink href="#">Welcome email</MenuLink>
      <MenuLink href="#">Order confirmation</MenuLink>
      <MenuLink href="#">Password reset</MenuLink>
    </MenuGroup>
    <MenuSeparator />
    <MenuGroup>
      <MenuLabel>Draft</MenuLabel>
      <MenuLink href="#">New promo campaign</MenuLink>
      <MenuLink href="#">Account deactivation</MenuLink>
    </MenuGroup>
  </MenuContent>
</Menu>
```

### Submenus
Use MenuSub, MenuSubTrigger, and MenuSubContent for nested menus.
```vue
<Menu>
  <MenuTrigger>
    <Button color="primary" size="lg" variant="ghost">Options <ChevronDownMd /></Button>
  </MenuTrigger>
  <MenuContent :min-width="180">
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}">Edit</MenuItem>
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}">Duplicate</MenuItem>
    <MenuSeparator />
    <MenuSub>
      <MenuSubTrigger>More</MenuSubTrigger>
      <MenuSubContent>
        <MenuItem @select="() => {}">Move to project…</MenuItem>
        <MenuItem @select="() => {}">Move to folder…</MenuItem>
        <MenuSeparator />
        <MenuItem @select="() => {}">Advanced options…</MenuItem>
      </MenuSubContent>
    </MenuSub>
    <MenuSeparator />
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}">Share</MenuItem>
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}">Add to favorites</MenuItem>
    <MenuSeparator />
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}">Delete</MenuItem>
  </MenuContent>
</Menu>
```

### Checkbox items
Use MenuCheckboxItem with v-model:checked and {'@select="(evt) => evt.preventDefault()"'} to keep the menu open.
```vue
<Menu>
  <MenuTrigger>
    <Button variant="ghost" color="primary">Checkbox menu <ChevronDownMd /></Button>
  </MenuTrigger>
  <MenuContent :min-width="200">
    <MenuCheckboxItem
      v-model:checked="settings.showGrid"
      :indicator-position="indicatorPosition"
      :indicator-variant="indicatorVariant"
      @select="(evt) => evt.preventDefault()"
    >
      Show grid lines
    </MenuCheckboxItem>
    <MenuCheckboxItem
      v-model:checked="settings.showLabels"
      :indicator-position="indicatorPosition"
      :indicator-variant="indicatorVariant"
      @select="(evt) => evt.preventDefault()"
    >
      Display labels
    </MenuCheckboxItem>
    <MenuCheckboxItem
      v-model:checked="settings.enableShadows"
      :indicator-position="indicatorPosition"
      :indicator-variant="indicatorVariant"
      @select="(evt) => evt.preventDefault()"
    >
      Enable shadows
    </MenuCheckboxItem>
  </MenuContent>
</Menu>
```

### Radio items
Use MenuRadioGroup and MenuRadioItem for single-selection options. Bind the selection with v-model:value.
```vue
<Menu>
  <MenuTrigger>
    <Button color="primary" variant="ghost">Radio menu <ChevronDownMd /></Button>
  </MenuTrigger>
  <MenuContent align="start" min-width="auto" width="auto">
    <MenuRadioGroup v-model:value="value" :indicator-position="indicatorPosition">
      <MenuRadioItem value="any">Any time</MenuRadioItem>
      <MenuRadioItem value="today" disabled>Today</MenuRadioItem>
      <MenuRadioItem value="last7d">Last 7 days</MenuRadioItem>
      <MenuRadioItem value="last30d">Last 30 days</MenuRadioItem>
      <MenuRadioItem value="last3m">Last 3 months</MenuRadioItem>
    </MenuRadioGroup>
  </MenuContent>
</Menu>
```

### Item actions
Use MenuItemActions and MenuItemAction for hover actions (e.g. delete) on an item.Accessibility note: these actions are not keyboard accessible due to how the menu primitive works, so they should not be critical actions with no other parity.
```vue
<Menu>
  <MenuTrigger>
    <Button color="primary" size="lg" variant="ghost" class="font-semibold gap-1.5"><History /> Conversations</Button>
  </MenuTrigger>
  <MenuContent :width="270">
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}">
      <span class="flex-1 truncate">Sample thread title</span>
      <MenuItemActions>
        <MenuItemAction @click="() => {}"><Trash /></MenuItemAction>
      </MenuItemActions>
    </MenuItem>
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}">
      <span class="flex-1 truncate">Another sample thread title</span>
      <MenuItemActions>
        <MenuItemAction @click="() => {}"><Trash /></MenuItemAction>
      </MenuItemActions>
    </MenuItem>
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}">
      <span class="flex-1 truncate">Long thread title that is going to be clipped off</span>
      <MenuItemActions>
        <MenuItemAction @click="() => {}"><Trash /></MenuItemAction>
      </MenuItemActions>
    </MenuItem>
  </MenuContent>
</Menu>
```

### Destructive items
Mark a destructive action (delete, remove, leave) with danger: the label and the highlight take the danger color, and a description keeps its secondary color. MenuLink takes danger too. Put such items last, after a separator.
```vue
<Menu>
  <MenuTrigger>
    <Button color="secondary" variant="soft" uniform aria-label="More actions">
      <DotsHorizontal />
    </Button>
  </MenuTrigger>
  <MenuContent align="end" :min-width="200">
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><EditPencil /> Rename</MenuItem>
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}">Duplicate</MenuItem>
    <MenuSeparator />
    <MenuItem @select="() => {}" danger><Trash /> Delete project</MenuItem>
  </MenuContent>
</Menu>
```

## OTP Input
Input for one-time passwords and verification codes, with split digit cells, grouped layouts, error states, and autofill.
Import: import { OTPInput } from "@plexui/vue/components/OTPInput"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/otp-input
Props:
  - length (number) default: 6 — Number of digit cells
  - value / v-model:value (string) — Controlled value; `update:value` fires on every change
  - @change ((value: string) => void) — Fires when the value changes
  - @complete ((value: string) => void) — Fires when all digits are filled
  - grouping (number[]) — Grouping pattern with extra gap (e.g. [3,3])
  - invalid (boolean) default: false — Invalid visual state
  - error-message (string) — Error message below the input
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable all cells
  - auto-focus (boolean) default: false — Auto-focus first cell on mount

### Overview
```vue
<OTPInput v-model:value="value" />
```

### Filled state
```vue
<OTPInput v-model:value="value" />
```

### Grouped (3+3)
```vue
<OTPInput v-model:value="value" :grouping="[3, 3]" />
```

### With error
```vue
<OTPInput v-model:value="value" invalid error-message="Invalid verification code." />
```

### Disabled
```vue
<OTPInput value="488030" disabled />
```

## Popover
A floating panel for contextual UI, with flexible sizing, hover triggers, a controllable open state and nesting.
Import: import { Popover, PopoverContent, PopoverTrigger } from "@plexui/vue/components/Popover"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/popover
Props:
  - showOnHover (boolean) — Open on hover and focus instead of click
  - open / v-model:open (boolean) — Controlled open state; uncontrolled when omitted
  PopoverContent:
    - width (number | string) — Panel width (px or CSS value)
    - minWidth (number | string) default: 300 — Minimum panel width in px
    - maxWidth (number | string) — Maximum panel width
    - side ("top" | "right" | "bottom" | "left") — Preferred placement
    - align ("start" | "center" | "end") — Alignment relative to trigger
    - sideOffset (number) — Offset from trigger (px)
    - alignOffset (number) — Alignment offset (px)

### Overview
```vue
<Popover>
  <PopoverTrigger>
    <Button color="primary" :pill="false">Generate</Button>
  </PopoverTrigger>
  <PopoverContent side="right">
    <div class="p-4">
      <p class="text-sm text-secondary">Popover content. Use with any floating UI.</p>
    </div>
  </PopoverContent>
</Popover>
```

### Sizing the popover
The popover can be sized with any combination of width, min-width, or max-width. All popovers default to a min-width of 300px, and a max-width of the popper's viewport detection.
```vue
<Popover>
  <PopoverTrigger>
    <Button color="primary" size="sm" variant="ghost" :pill="false" gutter-size="xs" class="gap-1.5">
      <Bolt /> Actions
    </Button>
  </PopoverTrigger>
  <PopoverContent class="flex p-1.5" min-width="auto" side="top">
    <Button color="primary" variant="ghost" :pill="false" size="sm" gutter-size="2xs">
      <Functions :height="18" :width="18" />
    </Button>
    <Button color="primary" variant="ghost" :pill="false" size="sm" gutter-size="2xs">
      <Search :height="18" :width="18" />
    </Button>
    <Button color="primary" variant="ghost" :pill="false" size="sm" gutter-size="2xs">
      <Globe :height="18" :width="18" />
    </Button>
    <Button color="primary" variant="ghost" :pill="false" size="sm" gutter-size="2xs">
      <Code :height="18" :width="18" />
    </Button>
    <Button color="primary" variant="ghost" :pill="false" size="sm" gutter-size="2xs">
      <ImageSquare :height="18" :width="18" />
    </Button>
  </PopoverContent>
</Popover>
```

### Open on hover
Use show-on-hover to create popovers that appear on hover and focus events. This should be used sparingly and with clear intention.By default, hover popovers will not alter the focused element on the page, nor restore focus to the trigger on close. However, if interactive content is present in the popover, and tab is used to navigate into the popover, focus will be restored to the trigger on close.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-4">
  <Popover show-on-hover>
    <PopoverTrigger>
      <Button color="primary" :pill="false">Static content</Button>
    </PopoverTrigger>
    <PopoverContent :width="320" side="right">
      <div class="p-3">
        <h3 class="mb-0.5 font-semibold">Popover Title</h3>
        <p class="text-sm text-secondary">
          This is an example of a hoverable popover containing presentational content.
        </p>
        <footer class="mt-2 text-xs text-tertiary">Last updated June 20, 2025</footer>
      </div>
    </PopoverContent>
  </Popover>
  <Popover show-on-hover>
    <PopoverTrigger>
      <Button color="primary" :pill="false">Interactive content</Button>
    </PopoverTrigger>
    <PopoverContent :min-width="230" side="right" v-slot="{ close }">
      <div class="p-3">
        <h3 class="mb-0.5 text-sm font-semibold">Popover with actions</h3>
        <p class="text-sm text-secondary">Try using tab to navigate</p>
        <div class="mt-3 flex gap-2">
          <Button color="secondary" variant="soft" :pill="false" @click="close">
            Cancel
          </Button>
          <Button color="primary" :pill="false" @click="close">
            Confirm
          </Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </PopoverContent>
  </Popover>
</div>
```

### Using the controller
The default slot of PopoverContent receives {"{ close, shake }"}. Components rendered inside it can get the same two methods from usePopoverController() in their setup(). They do exactly what you think they do.Attempting to submit an empty form calls the shake() methodAfter async submission occurs (submitPrompt() fakes a two-second request), the close() method is called to close the popup imperatively.
```vue
<Popover>
  <PopoverTrigger>
    <Button color="primary" :pill="false">Generate</Button>
  </PopoverTrigger>
  <PopoverContent side="right" v-slot="{ close, shake }">
    <form
      @submit.prevent="
        if (!prompt) return shake();
        submitting = true;
        submitPrompt().then(() => { submitting = false; prompt = ''; close(); });
      "
    >
      <textarea
        v-model="prompt"
        placeholder="Describe what you're using the model for, and we'll generate system instructions."
        style="width: 100%; height: 80px; background: none; padding: 12px 16px 8px; border: 0; font-size: 14px; line-height: 20px; resize: none; outline: 0"
      />
      <div class="px-3 pb-3 flex items-center justify-between">
        <div class="flex gap-1.5 items-center font-normal text-sm text-tertiary">
          <Sparkles :width="16" :height="16" />
          Free beta
        </div>
        <Button color="primary" size="xs" :pill="false" :loading="submitting" type="submit">
          Create
        </Button>
      </div>
    </form>
  </PopoverContent>
</Popover>
```

## Progress
A horizontal bar that shows task completion, with determinate and indeterminate modes, labelled layouts, and smooth value transitions.
Import: import { Progress } from "@plexui/vue/components/Progress"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/progress
Props:
  - value (number | null) default: null — Current progress between 0 and max. Pass null for indeterminate.
  - max (number) default: 100 — Maximum progress value
  - get-value-label ((value: number, max: number) => string) — Accessible value text (aria-valuetext). Defaults to the rounded percentage, e.g. "67%".
  - class (string) — Applied to the root <div>

### Overview
```vue
<script setup>
import { onMounted, onUnmounted, ref } from "vue"

const value = ref(13)
let timer
onMounted(() => {
  timer = setTimeout(() => (value.value = 66), 500)
})
onUnmounted(() => clearTimeout(timer))
</script>

<template>
  <Progress :value="value" />
</template>
```

### With label
Pair a Label with a value readout above the bar, the typical upload or onboarding progress pattern.
```vue
<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px; width: 100%; max-width: 320px">
  <div style="display: flex; align-items: baseline; justify-content: space-between; gap: 16px">
    <Label for="progress-upload">Uploading plex-ui.zip</Label>
    <span style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); font-size: 14px">3.2 / 4.8 MB</span>
  </div>
  <Progress id="progress-upload" :value="67" />
</div>
```

### Controlled
Bind value to any source, here a Slider through v-model:value.
```vue
<div style="display: flex; width: 100%; max-width: 320px; flex-direction: column; gap: 16px">
  <Progress :value="value" />
  <Slider v-model:value="value" :min="0" :max="100" :step="1" />
</div>
```

## Progress Steps
Stepper for multi-step flows like checkouts and onboarding, with horizontal or vertical layout, icons, navigation, and color tokens.
Import: import { ProgressSteps, ProgressStepsStep, ProgressStepsTitle, ProgressStepsDescription } from "@plexui/vue/components/ProgressSteps"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/progress-steps
Props:
  - current (number) — Current active step (1-based)
  - orientation ("horizontal" | "vertical") default: "horizontal" — Layout direction
  - size ("sm" | "md" | "lg") default: "md" — Step indicator size
  - color ("default" | "success") default: "default" — Color scheme for completed/active steps
  - connector-style ("solid" | "dashed") default: "solid" — Connector line style
  - variant ("minimal") — Compact bar variant (requires total)
  - total (number) — Total steps for minimal variant
  ProgressStepsStep:
    - icon (Component | VNode | string) — Custom icon instead of step number; the icon slot takes precedence

### Overview
```vue
<ProgressSteps
  :current="Number(current)"
  :orientation="orientation"
  :size="size"
  :color="color"
  :connector-style="connectorStyle"
  class="w-full"
>
  <ProgressStepsStep>
    <ProgressStepsTitle>Your details</ProgressStepsTitle>
    <ProgressStepsDescription>Please provide your name and email</ProgressStepsDescription>
  </ProgressStepsStep>
  <ProgressStepsStep>
    <ProgressStepsTitle>Company details</ProgressStepsTitle>
    <ProgressStepsDescription>A few details about your company</ProgressStepsDescription>
  </ProgressStepsStep>
  <ProgressStepsStep>
    <ProgressStepsTitle>Invite your team</ProgressStepsTitle>
    <ProgressStepsDescription>Start collaborating with your team</ProgressStepsDescription>
  </ProgressStepsStep>
  <ProgressStepsStep>
    <ProgressStepsTitle>Add your socials</ProgressStepsTitle>
    <ProgressStepsDescription>Share posts to your social accounts</ProgressStepsDescription>
  </ProgressStepsStep>
</ProgressSteps>
```

### Sizes
Use size to control the scale of step indicators.
```vue
<ProgressSteps :current="Number(current)" :size="size" class="w-full">
  <ProgressStepsStep><ProgressStepsTitle>Step 1</ProgressStepsTitle></ProgressStepsStep>
  <ProgressStepsStep><ProgressStepsTitle>Step 2</ProgressStepsTitle></ProgressStepsStep>
  <ProgressStepsStep><ProgressStepsTitle>Step 3</ProgressStepsTitle></ProgressStepsStep>
  <ProgressStepsStep><ProgressStepsTitle>Step 4</ProgressStepsTitle></ProgressStepsStep>
</ProgressSteps>
```

### Vertical
Use orientation="vertical" to display steps in a vertical layout, ideal for sidebar navigation or multi-step forms. The example also shows the dashed connector style. steps is an array of {"{ title, description }"} objects.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-20">
  <div>
    <div class="mb-4 text-tertiary text-sm">Default</div>
    <ProgressSteps :current="2" orientation="vertical">
      <ProgressStepsStep v-for="step in steps" :key="step.title">
        <ProgressStepsTitle>{{ step.title }}</ProgressStepsTitle>
        <ProgressStepsDescription>{{ step.description }}</ProgressStepsDescription>
      </ProgressStepsStep>
    </ProgressSteps>
  </div>
  <div>
    <div class="mb-4 text-tertiary text-sm">With dashed connector</div>
    <ProgressSteps :current="3" orientation="vertical" connector-style="dashed">
      <ProgressStepsStep v-for="step in steps" :key="step.title">
        <ProgressStepsTitle>{{ step.title }}</ProgressStepsTitle>
        <ProgressStepsDescription>{{ step.description }}</ProgressStepsDescription>
      </ProgressStepsStep>
    </ProgressSteps>
  </div>
</div>
```

### With Icons
Use the icon slot (or the icon prop) on ProgressStepsStep to display custom icons instead of step numbers.
```vue
<ProgressSteps :current="3" class="w-full">
  <ProgressStepsStep>
    <template #icon><Email /></template>
    <ProgressStepsTitle>Your details</ProgressStepsTitle>
    <ProgressStepsDescription>Please provide your name and email</ProgressStepsDescription>
  </ProgressStepsStep>
  <ProgressStepsStep>
    <template #icon><Globe /></template>
    <ProgressStepsTitle>Company details</ProgressStepsTitle>
    <ProgressStepsDescription>Website and location</ProgressStepsDescription>
  </ProgressStepsStep>
  <ProgressStepsStep>
    <template #icon><Users /></template>
    <ProgressStepsTitle>Invite your team</ProgressStepsTitle>
    <ProgressStepsDescription>Start collaborating</ProgressStepsDescription>
  </ProgressStepsStep>
  <ProgressStepsStep>
    <template #icon><Bolt /></template>
    <ProgressStepsTitle>Get started</ProgressStepsTitle>
    <ProgressStepsDescription>Ready to go</ProgressStepsDescription>
  </ProgressStepsStep>
</ProgressSteps>
```

### With Navigation
Example of integrating with navigation controls.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col items-center gap-6">
  <ProgressSteps variant="minimal" :current="step" :total="4" />
  <div class="text-center">
    <h3 class="text-lg font-semibold">Step {{ step }} of 4</h3>
    <p class="text-secondary mt-1">{{ steps[Math.min(step - 1, 3)].title }}</p>
  </div>
  <div class="flex gap-3">
    <Button color="secondary" variant="outline" class="w-28" :disabled="step === 1" @click="step = Math.max(1, step - 1)">
      Previous
    </Button>
    <Button color="primary" class="w-28" :disabled="step === 4" @click="step = Math.min(4, step + 1)">
      Next
    </Button>
  </div>
</div>
```

### Minimal
Use variant="minimal" for a compact horizontal bar indicator. Requires total prop.
```vue
<ProgressSteps variant="minimal" :current="Number(current)" :total="4" :color="color" />
```

### Icons Only
Omit ProgressStepsTitle and ProgressStepsDescription for a minimal indicator when step details are shown elsewhere.
```vue
<ProgressSteps :current="2" class="w-full">
  <ProgressStepsStep />
  <ProgressStepsStep />
  <ProgressStepsStep />
  <ProgressStepsStep />
</ProgressSteps>
```

### Colors
Use color to change the color scheme of completed and active steps.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-12 w-full">
  <div>
    <div class="mb-4 text-tertiary text-sm">Default</div>
    <ProgressSteps :current="3" class="w-full">
      <ProgressStepsStep><ProgressStepsTitle>Step 1</ProgressStepsTitle></ProgressStepsStep>
      <ProgressStepsStep><ProgressStepsTitle>Step 2</ProgressStepsTitle></ProgressStepsStep>
      <ProgressStepsStep><ProgressStepsTitle>Step 3</ProgressStepsTitle></ProgressStepsStep>
      <ProgressStepsStep><ProgressStepsTitle>Step 4</ProgressStepsTitle></ProgressStepsStep>
    </ProgressSteps>
  </div>
  <div>
    <div class="mb-4 text-tertiary text-sm">Success</div>
    <ProgressSteps :current="3" color="success" class="w-full">
      <ProgressStepsStep><ProgressStepsTitle>Step 1</ProgressStepsTitle></ProgressStepsStep>
      <ProgressStepsStep><ProgressStepsTitle>Step 2</ProgressStepsTitle></ProgressStepsStep>
      <ProgressStepsStep><ProgressStepsTitle>Step 3</ProgressStepsTitle></ProgressStepsStep>
      <ProgressStepsStep><ProgressStepsTitle>Step 4</ProgressStepsTitle></ProgressStepsStep>
    </ProgressSteps>
  </div>
</div>
```

## Radio Group
Radio group for single-choice selection with row or column layout, right-aligned indicators, rich item content, and card-style options.
Import: import { RadioGroup, RadioGroupItem } from "@plexui/vue/components/RadioGroup"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/radio-group
Props:
  - value (string) — Selected value. Bind with v-model:value; leave unset for an uncontrolled group
  - default-value (string) — Initial selected value (uncontrolled)
  - direction ("col" | "row") default: "row" — Item flow direction
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable the whole group
  - name (string) — Name of the hidden radio inputs submitted with the owning form
  - required (boolean) default: false — Marks the group as required (aria-required)
  - aria-label (string) — Accessible group label
  - class (string) — Extra classes on the group element
  - default slot (VNode[]) — RadioGroupItem children
  - @update:value ((value: string) => void) — Emitted when the selected option changes; powers v-model:value
  - @change ((value: string) => void) — Emitted with update:value, named after the React onChange prop
  RadioGroupItem:
    - value (string) — Item value
    - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable this item only
    - required (boolean) — Marks this item's hidden form input as required
    - block (boolean) default: false — Stretch item to fill available width
    - orientation ("left" | "right") default: "left" — Position of the radio indicator relative to the label
    - description (string) — Second line below the option's label, linked via aria-describedby (or the description slot)
    - class (string) — Extra classes on the label element
    - default slot (VNode[]) — Label content (text or rich content)

### Overview
```vue
<div>
  <h3 class="font-semibold text-sm mb-3">Notification frequency</h3>
  <RadioGroup direction="col" v-model:value="frequency" aria-label="Notification frequency">
    <RadioGroupItem value="daily">Daily</RadioGroupItem>
    <RadioGroupItem value="weekly">Weekly</RadioGroupItem>
    <RadioGroupItem value="monthly">Monthly</RadioGroupItem>
    <RadioGroupItem value="never">Never</RadioGroupItem>
  </RadioGroup>
</div>
```

### With Checkbox
Place RadioGroup alongside Checkbox controls to verify that indicator sizes, label baselines, and spacing align consistently in both horizontal and vertical layouts.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-6 w-full max-w-md">
  <Field label="Notification channel" description="Choose how you'd like to be notified." v-slot="{ id }">
    <RadioGroup :direction="direction" default-value="email" aria-label="Notification channel">
      <RadioGroupItem
        v-for="channel in [
          { value: 'email', label: 'Email', description: 'Messages delivered to your inbox.' },
          { value: 'sms', label: 'SMS', description: 'Text messages to your phone number.' },
          { value: 'push', label: 'Push', description: 'Real-time alerts on your device.' },
        ]"
        :key="channel.value"
        :value="channel.value"
      >
        <div v-if="description === 'true'" class="flex flex-col">
          <span class="font-medium">{{ channel.label }}</span>
          <span class="text-xs text-secondary mt-0.5">{{ channel.description }}</span>
        </div>
        <template v-else>{{ channel.label }}</template>
      </RadioGroupItem>
    </RadioGroup>
  </Field>
  <Field label="Topics" description="Select the topics you want to follow." v-slot="{ id }">
    <div :class="direction === 'row' ? 'flex flex-row gap-5' : 'flex flex-col gap-2.5'">
      <Checkbox
        v-for="topic in [
          { label: 'Product updates', description: 'New features and improvements.', checked: true },
          { label: 'Security alerts', description: 'Account activity and login notifications.', checked: true },
          { label: 'Community', description: 'Events, meetups, and discussions.', checked: false },
        ]"
        :key="topic.label"
        :default-checked="topic.checked"
      >
        <template #label>
          <div v-if="description === 'true'" class="flex flex-col">
            <span class="font-medium">{{ topic.label }}</span>
            <span class="text-xs text-secondary mt-0.5">{{ topic.description }}</span>
          </div>
          <template v-else>{{ topic.label }}</template>
        </template>
      </Checkbox>
    </div>
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Descriptions
Combine Field-level label and description with item-level descriptions for options that need additional context. Use a plain label without descriptions when the options are self-explanatory.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col max-w-sm w-full divide-y divide-alpha/15">
  <div class="pb-5">
    <Field label="Notification channel" description="Choose how you'd like to be notified." v-slot="{ id }">
      <RadioGroup direction="col" v-model:value="channel" aria-label="Notification channel">
        <RadioGroupItem value="email">
          <div class="flex flex-col">
            <span class="font-medium">Email</span>
            <span class="text-xs text-secondary mt-0.5">Messages delivered to your inbox.</span>
          </div>
        </RadioGroupItem>
        <RadioGroupItem value="sms">
          <div class="flex flex-col">
            <span class="font-medium">SMS</span>
            <span class="text-xs text-secondary mt-0.5">Text messages to your phone number.</span>
          </div>
        </RadioGroupItem>
        <RadioGroupItem value="push">
          <div class="flex flex-col">
            <span class="font-medium">Push</span>
            <span class="text-xs text-secondary mt-0.5">Real-time alerts on your device.</span>
          </div>
        </RadioGroupItem>
      </RadioGroup>
    </Field>
  </div>
  <div class="pt-5">
    <Field label="Notification frequency" v-slot="{ id }">
      <RadioGroup direction="col" v-model:value="frequency" aria-label="Notification frequency">
        <RadioGroupItem value="daily">Daily</RadioGroupItem>
        <RadioGroupItem value="weekly">Weekly</RadioGroupItem>
        <RadioGroupItem value="monthly">Monthly</RadioGroupItem>
        <RadioGroupItem value="never">Never</RadioGroupItem>
      </RadioGroup>
    </Field>
  </div>
</div>
```

### Direction
Use direction to control whether the items flow in a column or row. An optimal gap will be dynamically applied based on the orientation.
```vue
<RadioGroup :direction="direction" default-value="light" aria-label="Theme">
  <RadioGroupItem value="light">Light</RadioGroupItem>
  <RadioGroupItem value="dark">Dark</RadioGroupItem>
  <RadioGroupItem value="system">System</RadioGroupItem>
</RadioGroup>
```

### Orientation
Use orientation="right" on RadioGroupItem to place the radio indicator after the label text. Useful for settings lists and card-style selections where the content should lead.
```vue
<RadioGroup class="w-56" direction="col" default-value="email" aria-label="Default notification channel">
  <RadioGroupItem value="email" :orientation="orientation" block>Email</RadioGroupItem>
  <RadioGroupItem value="sms" :orientation="orientation" block>SMS</RadioGroupItem>
  <RadioGroupItem value="push" :orientation="orientation" block>Push notification</RadioGroupItem>
</RadioGroup>
```

### Group disabled
Use disabled on RadioGroup to disable all radio options.
```vue
<RadioGroup :disabled="disabled" default-value="standard" aria-label="Shipping method">
  <RadioGroupItem value="standard">Standard</RadioGroupItem>
  <RadioGroupItem value="express">Express</RadioGroupItem>
  <RadioGroupItem value="overnight">Overnight</RadioGroupItem>
</RadioGroup>
```

### Item disabled
Use disabled on a single RadioGroupItem to disable only that option.
```vue
<RadioGroup aria-label="Delivery method" class="max-w-[320px] m-auto" direction="col">
  <RadioGroupItem value="standard">Standard shipping (3–5 days)</RadioGroupItem>
  <RadioGroupItem value="express" :disabled="disabled">Express shipping (1–2 days)</RadioGroupItem>
  <RadioGroupItem value="same-day">
    Same-day delivery, available in select metro areas with a minimum order of $50
  </RadioGroupItem>
</RadioGroup>
```

### With description
Use description (or the description slot) on an item for a second line under its label. It sits outside the label, so the radio keeps the label as its name and gets the description through aria-describedby; clicking it does not select the option.
```vue
<RadioGroup direction="col" v-model:value="value" aria-label="Spacing">
  <RadioGroupItem value="default" description="Standard spacing for most use cases.">Default</RadioGroupItem>
  <RadioGroupItem value="comfortable" description="More space between elements.">Comfortable</RadioGroupItem>
  <RadioGroupItem value="compact" description="Minimal spacing for dense layouts.">Compact</RadioGroupItem>
</RadioGroup>
```

### With Field
Wrap a RadioGroup in a Field for a shared label and description. The Field provides aria-describedby linking automatically.
```vue
<div class="max-w-sm">
  <Field label="Notification method" description="Choose how you want to receive notifications." v-slot="{ id }">
    <RadioGroup direction="col" v-model:value="value" aria-label="Notification method">
      <RadioGroupItem value="email">Email</RadioGroupItem>
      <RadioGroupItem value="sms">SMS</RadioGroupItem>
      <RadioGroupItem value="push">Push notification</RadioGroupItem>
    </RadioGroup>
  </Field>
</div>
```

### With Field error
When a Field has an error-message, it sets aria-invalid on the child control for screen readers. The error message is displayed below the group.
```vue
<div class="max-w-sm">
  <Field
    label="Subscription plan"
    description="Select a plan to continue."
    :error-message="showError ? 'Please select a subscription plan.' : undefined"
    v-slot="fieldProps"
  >
    <RadioGroup direction="col" v-model:value="value" aria-label="Subscription plan" v-bind="fieldProps">
      <RadioGroupItem value="monthly">Monthly ($9.99/month)</RadioGroupItem>
      <RadioGroupItem value="yearly">Yearly ($99.99/year)</RadioGroupItem>
      <RadioGroupItem value="lifetime">Lifetime ($299.99)</RadioGroupItem>
    </RadioGroup>
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Choice card
Style each radio option as a bordered card with visual selection state. The selected card gets a highlighted border using design tokens. Use orientation="right" to place the indicator after the content. Wrap each RadioGroupItem in a card container with border and borderRadius on the same element.
```vue
<RadioGroup class="flex-col w-[320px] gap-3" v-model:value="value" aria-label="Workspace type">
  <div
    v-for="card in [
      { id: 'personal', title: 'Personal', description: 'For hobby projects and experimentation.' },
      { id: 'team', title: 'Team', description: 'Collaborate with up to 10 members.' },
      { id: 'business', title: 'Business', description: 'SSO, audit logs, and priority support.' },
    ]"
    :key="card.id"
    :style="{
      border: '1px solid',
      borderColor: value === card.id ? 'var(--color-background-primary-solid)' : 'var(--color-border-primary-outline)',
      borderRadius: 'var(--radius-lg)',
      backgroundColor: value === card.id ? 'var(--color-background-primary-soft-alpha)' : undefined,
      transition: 'border-color 150ms ease, background-color 150ms ease',
    }"
  >
    <RadioGroupItem :value="card.id" block :orientation="orientation" class="gap-3 p-3">
      <div class="flex flex-col flex-1">
        <span class="font-semibold">{{ card.title }}</span>
        <span class="text-xs text-secondary mt-0.5">{{ card.description }}</span>
      </div>
    </RadioGroupItem>
  </div>
</RadioGroup>
```

### Card selection
Leverage component composition and class overrides to build card-style radio selections with rich content per option.
```vue
<RadioGroup class="flex-col w-[390px] gap-4" aria-label="Sample options">
  <RadioGroupItem class="gap-2.5" value="basic">
    <div>
      <h4 class="font-semibold mb-1">Basic Plan</h4>
      <p class="text-secondary text-sm">5 GB storage • Email support • Free</p>
    </div>
  </RadioGroupItem>
  <hr class="border-default" />
  <RadioGroupItem class="gap-2.5" value="standard">
    <div>
      <h4 class="font-semibold mb-1">Standard Plan</h4>
      <p class="text-secondary text-sm">100 GB storage • Priority email support • $9.99/m</p>
    </div>
  </RadioGroupItem>
  <hr class="border-default" />
  <Tooltip :max-width="258" interactive>
    <template #content>
      Contact our <TextLink href="#">support team</TextLink> for more information on enterprise plans.
    </template>
    <TooltipTrigger>
      <RadioGroupItem class="gap-2.5" value="enterprise" disabled>
        <div>
          <h4 class="font-semibold mb-1">Enterprise Plan</h4>
          <p class="text-secondary text-sm">Unlimited storage • 24/7 phone support • Custom pricing</p>
        </div>
      </RadioGroupItem>
    </TooltipTrigger>
  </Tooltip>
</RadioGroup>
```

### Grouped sections
Use dividers to separate radio groups into logical sections. Each section can be wrapped in a Field with its own label and description.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col max-w-sm w-full divide-y divide-alpha/15">
  <div class="pb-5">
    <Field label="Visibility" description="Control who can see project resources." v-slot="{ id }">
      <RadioGroup direction="col" v-model:value="visibility" aria-label="Visibility">
        <RadioGroupItem value="hidden">Hidden</RadioGroupItem>
        <RadioGroupItem value="owners">Visible to organization owners</RadioGroupItem>
        <RadioGroupItem value="everyone">Visible to everyone</RadioGroupItem>
      </RadioGroup>
    </Field>
  </div>
  <div class="pt-5">
    <Field label="Digest frequency" description="How often you receive summary emails." v-slot="{ id }">
      <RadioGroup direction="col" v-model:value="frequency" aria-label="Digest frequency">
        <RadioGroupItem value="daily">Daily</RadioGroupItem>
        <RadioGroupItem value="weekly">Weekly</RadioGroupItem>
        <RadioGroupItem value="monthly">Monthly</RadioGroupItem>
        <RadioGroupItem value="never">Never</RadioGroupItem>
      </RadioGroup>
    </Field>
  </div>
</div>
```

## Select
Select dropdown with grouped options, per-item descriptions, multiple selection, action items, and column-based layouts.
Import: import { Select } from "@plexui/vue/components/Select"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/select
Props:
  - options (Options<T>) — Array of options or grouped options
  - v-model:value (string | string[]) — Selected value (single) or values (multiple)
  - @change ((option | options) => void) — Called when selection changes, with the selected option (or options with multiple)
  - multiple (boolean) default: false — Allow multiple selection
  - placeholder (string) default: "Select..." — Trigger placeholder
  - variant ("soft" | "outline" | "ghost") default: "outline" — Trigger variant
  - size (ControlSize) default: "md" — Trigger size (3xs to 3xl)
  - pill (boolean) default: false — Rounded trigger
  - clearable (boolean) default: false — Show clear button
  - block (boolean) default: variant !== "ghost" — Full width trigger
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable select
  - loading (boolean) default: false — Loading state; shows loading-placeholder and keeps the menu closed
  - actions (array) — Actions below options list
  - check-position ("start" | "end" | "none") default: "start" — Where the check indicator sits in the list. "none" removes the indicator column for lists that never carry a selection
  - #optionView / OptionView (slot (option) | Component) — Custom option render
  - #triggerView / TriggerView (slot | Component) — Custom trigger render: the selected option, or { values, selectedAll } with multiple
  - #trigger / trigger (slot ({ open, onToggle }) | function) — Custom trigger element, wrapped as the popover trigger
  - #searchEmptyMessage / search-empty-message (slot | string) default: "No results found." — Shown when the search matches nothing
  - search-placeholder (string) — Search input placeholder
  - search-aria-label (string) default: "Search options" — Accessible name of the search input, independent of the trigger label
  - search-size (ControlSize) default: "md" — Size of the search input in the menu, such as 2xl for 44px touch targets on phones
  - search-predicate ((option, term) => boolean) — Filters options while searching

### Overview
Bind the selection with v-model:value: a string, or an array of strings with multiple. The @change event carries the selected option (or options), like React's onChange.
```vue
<div style="width: 200px">
  <Select
    v-if="multiple"
    key="multi"
    placeholder="Select fruits..."
    :options="fruitsOptions"
    name="fruits"
    multiple
    :variant="variant"
    :size="size"
    :clearable="clearable"
    :check-position="checkPosition"
    v-model:value="multiFruits"
  >
    <template #triggerView="{ values, selectedAll }">{{ selectedAll ? "All fruits" : values.length === 1 ? values[0].label : values.length + " fruits" }}</template>
  </Select>
  <Select
    v-else
    key="single"
    placeholder="Select a fruit..."
    :options="fruitsOptions"
    name="fruits"
    :multiple="false"
    :variant="variant"
    :size="size"
    :clearable="clearable"
    :check-position="checkPosition"
    v-model:value="fruit"
  />
</div>
```

### With descriptions
Options carrying a description field render a secondary line under each label. A description is a string, a VNode or a render function; here each role's is a render function returning {''}. The title stays semibold in every state (idle, highlighted, selected): the component handles that automatically, no option-class-name needed. Pass a component to TriggerStartIcon to reflect the selected option's identity in the trigger.
```vue
<div style="width: 220px">
  <Select
    v-model:value="role"
    :options="roles"
    placeholder="Select role..."
    align="start"
    :list-min-width="260"
    :variant="variant"
    :size="size"
    :check-position="checkPosition"
    :TriggerStartIcon="triggerIcon ? (role === 'owner' ? UserLock : User) : undefined"
  />
</div>
```

### Actions
Custom, arbitrary actions can be displayed below the options list via the actions prop. Each action has an id, a label, an optional Icon component and className, and an onSelect callback.
```vue
<div style="width: 150px">
  <Select
    variant="soft"
    v-model:value="value"
    block
    :options="items"
    placeholder="Select..."
    align="start"
    :list-min-width="240"
    :actions="[
      { id: 'create', label: 'Create project', Icon: Plus, onSelect: () => {} },
      { id: 'overview', label: 'Organization overview', Icon: Workspace, onSelect: () => {} },
    ]"
  />
</div>
```

### Grouped options
Select supports option groups, similar to the native &lt;optgroup&gt; element, and per-option tooltips. All selects are searchable by default, but will only present a searchbox when it makes sense. Currently, this means when there are more than 15 options.
```vue
<div style="width: 200px">
  <Select
    variant="outline"
    v-model:value="value"
    side="bottom"
    size="lg"
    :options="groupedItems"
    :list-min-width="300"
    search-placeholder="Select an option..."
    clearable
  />
</div>
```

### Multiple selection
Multiple selection is supported through the multiple property. This changes how v-model:value, @change, and the #triggerView slot are typed, accepting and returning arrays of values.
```vue
<div style="width: 200px">
  <Select
    variant="soft"
    placeholder="Select fruits..."
    :options="fruitsOptions"
    name="fruits"
    multiple
    clearable
    v-model:value="fruits"
  >
    <template #triggerView="{ values, selectedAll }">{{ selectedAll ? "All fruits" : values.length === 1 ? values[0].label : values.length + " fruits" }}</template>
  </Select>
</div>
```

### Column configuration
